金融市場投資週報                 2026 年 10 月 05 日
上週重點訊息
◼ 美國製造業維持擴張，成本壓力升高：9 月 ISM 製造業調查顯示，美國製造業連續第九個月
位於擴張區間，新訂單與就業分項均見改善，反映接單與用人需求仍有支撐。然而，價格指數
由 8 月的 71.1 升至 9 月的 77.9，能源、金屬及關稅相關成本上升，加上供應商交貨偏慢，企
業面臨更大成本壓力 。 後續須觀察企業能否轉嫁成本 ， 以及通膨黏性對聯準會政策判斷的影響 。
◼ 日本短觀顯示製造業改善、服務業略緩：日本銀行 10 月 1 日公布的 9 月短觀顯示，大型製造

業景氣判斷指數由 6 月的＋22 升至＋24，大型非製造業則由＋37 降至＋35。企業預估 12 月
兩項指數分別回落至＋21 與＋30，顯示現況仍有支撐，後續看法則偏審慎。製造業改善有助
支撐日股獲利預期，但服務業放緩及日銀政策路徑仍須持續觀察。
◼ 台灣製造業活動加速擴張，AI 需求續強：10 月 1 日公布的製造業調查顯示，台灣 9 月製造業
活動擴張速度創 2021 年 8 月以來最快，AI 相關應用需求仍是重要推力。國發會 9 月 29 日公
布的 8 月景氣對策信號續呈紅燈，綜合判斷分數為 41 分，與 7 月持平，領先及同時指標皆續
升。科技供應鏈景氣仍有支撐，但成長動能能否擴及其他產業，仍是觀察後續景氣的重點。
市場展望
◼ AI 需求持續擴張，關注財報季表現： AI 基礎建設需求未受利率環境影響，而是持續擴張，帶
動光通訊、CCL、ABF 載板 、記憶體、先進製程與封裝等關鍵供應鏈維持強勁成長，市場表
現由全面上漲轉向結構性分化。隨新一代 AI 伺服器放量、CSP 資本支出維持高檔，具供給優

勢、定價能力及訂單能見度延伸至 2027 年的龍頭廠商仍可望受惠。10 月中旬起陸續公布的
第三季財報，若結果持續表現亮眼，可望為市場再添動能，屆時具 AI 供應鏈題材的台股與日
股亦可望受惠。
◼ 債券佈局聚焦 5~10 年期美國公債及投等債：雖然貨幣政策風險 下降有助於穩定長期通膨預
期，但政府與企業發債供給增加所帶來的排擠效應，以及美國經濟成長展現韌性，仍可能使長
天期利率面臨上行壓力。因此，在當前利率環境下，5~10 年期債券較能兼顧票息收益與存續
期間風險管理。投資級債方面，彭博美國投資級公司債指數利差約 80-90 個基點，雖然利差
偏窄，但高基準利率環境仍提供具吸引力的絕對收益水準，因此現階段5~10 年期美國公債及
投資級債的風險報酬結構相對較佳。

## Document 120: 261027_ms_lotes-UG

Source PDF: all report pdf/261027_ms_lotes-UG.pdf
Report source: line報告備份

### Page 1

M
Idea
3Q GM likely to decline on higher costs and slower new product adoption…
Lotes reported unaudited 3Q26 revenue of NT$9,924M (+4% q/q, +18% y/y). Higher
copper, gold and plastics costs for legacy products, coupled with slower new
product adoption, should continue to pressure GM. We forecast 3Q GM of 44.6% (-
90bps q/q), below consensus of 45.8% (+30bps q/q). However, a decline in opex to
NT$2.0B, consistent with management commentary, should support operating

profit growth of 17% q/q to NT$2,452M, albeit still 6% below consensus. We
forecast NP of NT$2,174M (+10% q/q) and EPS of NT$19.36, 4% below the Street.
…but we see 3Q as the GM trough, with recovery beginning in 4Q: Following
slower  server platform transitions, we expect adoption to accelerate from 4Q26.
Socket production for AMD's Venice CPU will ramp from 4Q26 and socket
production for Intel’s Oak Stream CPU will ramp in 1Q27. These transitions should

enable Lotes to reprice products and pass through higher input costs, supporting
margin recovery. Our industry checks also point to a rebound in ODM server board
production in 4Q, following a modest 3Q decline. We forecast 4Q revenue of ~NT
$10.5B (+5% q/q), GM of 45.9% (+130bps q/q) and EPS of NT$21.11 (+9% q/q).
2027 - margin recovery and new AI product ramps should drive earnings growth:
Rising adoption of new CPU platforms should support socket margin recovery,

alongside growing contributions from QD, SOCAMM and NPO. QD reached 2–3% of
revenue in September, with further growth expected in 4Q26 and 2027, and we
forecast SOCAMM will reach ~7% of revenue in CY27. Lotes has two NPO
opportunities, one through LINTES and another directly, with the direct program
expected to ramp in 2H27. Although initial contributions should remain modest, we
see NPO as a longer-term AI data center growth opportunity and a potential re-

rating catalyst. Overall, we see the AI revenue mix, including QD, SOCAMM and
NPO, rising from ~5% in CY25 to slightly above 20% by CY28.
Upgrade to OW and raise PT by 15% to NT$2,350, driven by our estimate increases
for CY27-28. Our new PT implies 22x 2027e P/E and 16.5x 2028e P/E, above the past
3-year average of 17.4x, which we view as reasonable amid a rising AI revenue mix.

3Q26 Results Preview
3Q26 preview:  We forecast revenue of NT$9.92B (+4% q/q, +18% y/y), broadly in line
with the Street. Although gross margin should decline 80bps q/q to 44.6%, a 12%
sequential reduction in operating expenses should lift operating margin 270bps q/q to
24.7%. Nevertheless, this remains 130bps below consensus, implying operating profit of
NT$2.45bn, 6% below the Street. We therefore see potential for an earnings miss, as we

estimate net profit of ~NT$2,174M (+10% q/q, -6% y/y), for EPS of NT$19.36, 4% below
the Street.

AI revenue mix - a sharp step-up in 2027 should strengthen the structural growth
story: We forecast AI revenue contribution will rise from ~5% in CY25  to slightly above

20% by CY28, with the largest increase occurring in 2027. Rising adoption of QD and
SOCAMM connectors should drive the initial acceleration, while NPO sockets provide an
additional growth opportunity, with small-volume contributions expected in 2027 and
larger volumes in 2028. We believe this expanding AI exposure, alongside expected margin
recovery in CPU sockets, should strengthen Lotes’ earnings growth profile and support a
potential valuation re-rating as investors look beyond near-term cost pressures.

### Page 6

Idea
6
Price Target Discussion and Valuation Methodology
Our price target increases ~15%, to NT$2,350 from NT$2,045, driven mainly by our
higher earnings estimates for 2027-28.
Our new PT implies ~22x 2027e P/E, slightly above its three-year average forward P/E of
17.4x, but still below that of peer Amphenol (25-35x 2027e P/E, based on Visible Alpha
consensus). We think Lotes stock could  re-rate to a higher valuation multiple if margins
hold up well and Lotes' exposure to AI PCs and AI servers grows in the coming years.

We continue to derive our PT, which is our base case scenario value, from a residual
income model (RIM), consistent with the methodology we apply to the rest of our tech
hardware coverage universe. To calculate the intrinsic value of the stock, we use the
company's beginning equity plus the present value of all expected residual income of
earnings in excess of the cost of capital. The RI is positive when ROAE is above the cost of
capital and negative when it is below the cost of capital.

Our key RIM assumptions (all unchanged) include a cost of equity of 9.2% (beta of ~1.0,
equity risk premium of ~8.7% and risk-free rate of ~1.0%), a medium-term growth rate of
12.5% and a terminal growth rate of 3%.
Our bull and bear case values rise to NT$3,400 and NT1,285, respectively, in line with
the increase in magnitude of our base case scenario.

Hold or Sell to the stocks we cover. Overweight, Equal-weight, Not-Rated and Underweight are not the equivalent of buy, hold and sell.  Investors should carefully read the definitions of all
ratings used in Morgan Stanley Research. In addition, since Morgan Stanley Research contains more complete information concerning the analyst's views, investors should carefully read Morgan

Stanley Research, in its entirety, and not infer the contents from the rating alone.  In any case, ratings (or research) should not be used or relied upon as investment advice.  An investor's decision
to buy or sell a stock should depend on individual circumstances (such as the investor's existing holdings) and other considerations.

Analyst Stock Ratings
Overweight (O). The stock's total return is expected to exceed the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next
12-18 months.
Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

## Document 121: 261027_ms_macronix

### Page 1

M
Update
Macronix International Co Ltd  | Asia Pacific
Pricing hike lifts margin further;
shifting from NOR toward
NAND
3Q earnings affected by capacity reallocation:
• 3Q revenue: NT$24.2bn, +27% Q/Q, +195% Y/Y, 9% below our estimate; we
cite to capacity transition from NOR to NAND.
• Gross margin: 70.5%, +6.1ppt Q/Q, +57.0ppt Y/Y, 2.7ppt above our estimate –
helped by ~NT$750mn inventory reversal, mostly associated with NOR..
• EPS: NT$5.52, +41% Q/Q, 13% below our estimate.

•  Employee compensation accruals led to a 24% Q/Q operating expense
increase.
Shifting focus to NAND: NOR revenue increased 21% Q/Q despite a 12% Q/Q
decline in bit shipments, consistent with pricing strength. Management attributed
lower NOR shipments to capacity reallocation toward NAND; NAND revenue rose
39% Q/Q, mainly from pricing.
The first phase of capacity expansion is scheduled to enter volume production in
2H27; the additional NT$15.8bn investment could contribute in 2028.

Incremental capacity will favor NAND, in particular 3D NAND-based eMMC (i.e., MLC
NAND), with no planned increase in NOR capacity. The company will gradually
withdraw from ROM product.

### Page 3

Update
Morgan Stanley Research 3
Valuation Methodology and Risks
Macronix International Co Ltd (2337.TW)
Base case, P/B, in line with our Greater China memory coverage, due to the industry's cyclical
nature. We apply a target multiple of 2.1x to our 2027 BVPS estimate vs. its historical average
of 1.5x since 2017. We continue to believe the P/B target is justifiable, as the MLC and legacy
TLC NAND could be in greater shortage into 2H26, with under-supply easily reaching 40%. We

believe the only supplier that could fill the gap remains Macronix.
Risks to Upside
n Wider legacy NAND supply/demand gap
n NOR up-cycle, driven by limited supply expansion and/or stronger demand
n Faster growth for Switch hardware and core titles
Risks to Downside
n Less severe legacy NAND supply/demand gap
n NOR down-cycle, driven by faster supply expansion and/or demand weakness
n Slower growth for Switch hardware and core titles

Equal-weight (E). The stock's total return is expected to be in line with the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over

the next 12-18 months.
Not-Rated (NR). Currently the analyst does not have adequate conviction about the stock's total return relative to the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage
universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next 12-18 months.
Underweight (U). The stock's total return is expected to be below the average total return of the analyst's industry (or industry team's) coverage universe, on a risk-adjusted basis, over the next

12-18 months.
Unless otherwise specified, the time frame for price targets included in Morgan Stanley Research is 12 to 18 months.
Analyst Industry Views
Attractive (A): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be attractive vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated
below.
In-Line (I): The analyst expects the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months to be in line with the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.

Cautious (C): The analyst views the performance of his or her industry coverage universe over the next 12-18 months with caution vs. the relevant broad market benchmark, as indicated below.
Benchmarks for each region are as follows: North America - S&P 500; Latin America - relevant MSCI country index or MSCI Latin America Index; Europe - MSCI Europe; Japan - TOPIX; Asia -
relevant MSCI country index or MSCI sub-regional index or MSCI AC Asia Pacific ex Japan Index.

## Document 144: 261029_daiwa_ASE

Source PDF: all report pdf/261029_daiwa_ASE.pdf
Report source: line報告備份
Date: 2026-10-29
Pages: 7
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Taiwan Information Technology

What’s new: ASE disclosed a strong set of quarterly results and guidance 
on 29 October, both beating market expectations, thanks to the strong AI 
scaling which is just getting stronger to benefit ASE’s leading-edge 
advanced packaging (LEAP) business. We raise our forecasts and 12M TP 
and reaffirm our Outperform (2) call on the stock; accumulate into any 
technical pullback.

What’s the impact: Results and guidance all beat. ASE reported 3Q26 
net profit of c.TWD27bn, beating our/consensus estimates by 25%/18% 
thanks to higher semiconductor assembly & test (SAT, or IC ATM by 
company definition) revenue which came in at TWD151bn (USD4.7bn), 7% 
above our estimate, for higher gross margin (GM) of 30.5%, 1.9pp above 
ours and topping ASE’s structural target of 24-30%. The strength was 
amplified further by lower non-operating losses, resulting in the strong 
bottom-line beat (table on p.2). Guidance was another beat thanks to the 
strong SAT business where ASE expects revenue to grow 11-13% QoQ for 
GM to top its record of 30.5% during the COVID upturn. We raise our 2026-
28E EPS by 14-24% assuming its SAT GM to reach 33% through 2027.

Strong AI gets stronger. Discussions centred on ASE’s LEAP business, 
where management further hiked revenue target this year beyond USD4bn 
(USD1.6bn in 2025), after 3 hikes YTD, thanks to strong demand for its on-
substrate (oS) packaging and initial ramp of its full-process solutions. With 
addition of new co-packaged optics (CPO) solutions, ASE expects the 
LEAP revenue to double YoY in 2027. As LEAP is margin-accretive, ASE 
expects the SAT GM to continue improving beyond 4Q26, making the 
ceiling of its structural GM target now as the floor to kick off 2027 for a 
strong start. Given management’s emphasis on “friendly pricing”, we expect 
likely continued cost passthrough across the supply chain to add to GM 
upside. Working hard to narrow the supply/demand gap, ASE further raised 
this year’s capex to USD11.5bn (from USD10.5bn), reinforcing our view 
that AI is scaling strongly to accelerate the chip industry growth.

What we recommend: Given our forecast revisions, we raise our 12M TP 
for ASE to TWD710 (previous: TWD550), based on our revised ROE-
adjusted PBR target of 5.8x (previous: 5.2x) 2027E BVPS due to a higher 
ROE trajectory. Our revised TP translates into 24x 4-quarter forward PER, 
which we see as justifying the rich PBR target given its 2026-28E EPS 
CAGR of 25%. We reiterate our Outperform (2) call with key downside risks 
of any “silicon inflation” and geopolitical overhang leading to AI capex cut.

How we differ: Our 2026-27E EPS are 5-10% above the consensus as we 
believe the street has yet to factor in its stronger LEAP business.

29 October 2026

Entering an even stronger earnings cycle (III)

➢ 3Q26 results and 4Q26 guidance all beat expectations 
➢ Strong AI scaling is getting even stronger to benefit LEAP 
➢ Reaffirm Outperform (2) call with a higher 12M TP of TWD710

Company profile 
Established in April 2018, ASE Industrial Holding Co (HoldCo) is a holding company which privatized 
ASE and SPIL fully on 30 April 2018 through equity and cash swap, respectively, and listed on the same 
day with senior management assigned mainly by ASE. HoldCo has become the strong No.1 OSAT 
maker in the world, offering a comprehensive product portfolio to its customers from wirebonding, flip-
chip & bumping, system-in-package (SiP) to broad-based electronics manufacturing services (EMS).

### Page 5

5

ASE Technology Holding (3711 TT): 29 October 2026 
ESG analysis

Update Date: 5 Feb 2026 
Source: Daiwa, Company

ESG risks
Risks Management Analyst comments
G
Executive/board
quality 2
ASE’s Board of Directors consists of nine members with diverse professional backgrounds.
Three of the directors are independent, representing 33 percent of the board. The Chairman also
serves as the Chief Executive Officer, resulting in unified leadership and centralized decision-
making. To maintain effective governance and oversight under this structure, ASE relies on its
independent directors and established board processes. The board currently includes one
female director, and the company continues to pursue long-term goals related to board diversity
and balanced representation. ASE has an Audit Committee, Compensation Committee, Risk
Management Committee, and Chief Government Officer to conduct effective supervision.
Capital management 3
In the past 5 years, ASE paid over 50% or nearly 50% of its earnings in dividends. However, the
dividend trend was volatile in dollar and payout ratio terms. We forecast its dividend payout ratio
to average over 50% in the next 2 years.
Related party &
transaction 2
ASE undertakes related-party transactions with its subsidiaries on sales and purchases. The
total related-party transaction sales/purchase amounts were less than 10% of its total
sales/purchases, and the transaction terms with related parties were normal compared with
those with other customers/suppliers. Thus, we do not think related-party transactions have a
meaningful impact on overall operations.
S Supply chainmanagement 2
The supply of raw materials has the most direct impact on ASE's operations and manufacturing.
To lower overall supply-chain risks, ASE has expanded the scope of sustainability risk
management to non-tier-1 suppliers. ASE currently has over 600 non-tier-1 suppliers.
ASE requires its suppliers to comply with its Supplier Code of Conduct, which aims to ensure
that suppliers conduct business in a manner that meets labour, health & safety, environment,
business ethics & management, and various corporate compliance standards.
E Water & wastewatermanagement 2
ASE has adopted 3 water-use strategies of reduce, reuse and recycle. Through its water
management measures, it reduced water consumption density (water consumption/revenue) by
43% in 2024 vs. that of 2015. ASE is committed to increasing water reuse at its facilities in order
to improve water efficiency.
E
Waste & hazardous
materials
management
1
ASE is committed to improving waste recycling and reuse through the 5 key elements of a
circular economy of: direct recycling, reuse, off-site regeneration, renewable materials, and
supply and rental. The recycling rate of general waste was 90% in 2024 and its hazardous waste
density declined by 53% in 2024 vs. that of 2015.
E GHG emissions 1
ASE is committed to achieving net zero emissions by advancing low carbon energy adoption,
developing smart grid technologies, and strengthening high tech energy management systems.
As of 2024, 19% of total manufacturing energy consumption comes from renewable sources,
88% of ASE facilities use renewable energy, and 10 sites have achieved RE100 certification.
Note: Management score represents a company's ability to manage/benefit from certain ESG topics. The scores range from 1 to 3, with 1 being the strongest.

What surprised us 
AirTAC reported 3Q26 results today (October 29th). 3Q OP was in 
line with our estimates, but was 5% higher than BBG consensus. 
OPM was 33.9% in 3Q26, largely in line with our estimates; although 
GM of 49.4% was higher than our estimates by 0.9ppt, it was offset 
by higher opex (NT$1,778mn vs. GSe of NT$1,669mn). Total 
revenue was largely in line with GSe but was 4% above consensus, as 
the company enjoyed solid demand from the electronics industry 
(33% YoY in 3Q26 vs. 20% YoY in 2Q26, which accounted for 28% of 
total 3Q26 revenue). 
What surprised us the most during the results call was the 
company’s solid guidance on industry growth (mentioned low single 
to mid single digit % YoY growth is sustainable for the longer term 
for the China pneumatic market) and the company further 
mentioned that it could outgrow the market by 10ppt YoY in 2027. 
Moreover, the company expects all 
electronics/automotive/machine tools/general 
machinery/textile/packaging to see double digit YoY growth in both 
2026/27, thanks to the China government’s ongoing stimulus 
policies. Management reiterated 2026 revenue guidance in Rmb 
terms to reach 20%+ YoY growth (could reach higher YoY in NT$ 
terms), and maintained 2026 OPM guidance of 34%.  
The company reiterated its utilization rate target for its linear guide 
business to reach 50% by 2026 end (vs. 40%+ in 3Q26). The 
company continues to see peers raise prices for their linear guide 
products, and management thinks the company is well-positioned 
to gain market share with its stable pricing and shorter product lead 
time (less than a month vs. a key competitor at 4 months, see here).  
Going into 4Q26, we expect revenue to reach NT$11,425mn (-1% 
QoQ,+26% YoY), as we expect solid YoY across nearly all end 
markets. Rising raw material cost remains a key risk, while the

### Page 3

company continues to maintain a stable pricing strategy within the China market (which 
accounts for 90%+ of total revenue); thus we expect GM to slightly decline to 48.7% 
(-0.7ppt QoQ and +1.2ppt YoY). We continue to see the company’s ongoing effort in 
improving internal efficiency, which could partially offset the negative impact from rising 
material cost. 
We hold our conservative though optimistic view on the pneumatic industry outlook, as 
we continue to see downside risk in consumer electronics shipments (smartphone, etc) 
and the negative impact from rising raw material cost to persist over the coming 
quarters. We expect the company to continue to gain share in the China pneumatic 
market with its stable pricing strategy as well as its shift towards higher-quality 
pneumatic products.

Investment view 
We reiterate our Buy rating on AirTAC as we expect the company will continue to 
outperform the overall pneumatic market. We are positive on the company’s latest 
comment that the overall China pneumatic market YoY growth could be sustained at low 
single to mid single digit for a longer period, and management further mentioned that 
the company will outperform the market by 10ppt in 2027. This is mainly driven by the 
company’s latest guidance that nearly all end markets could see double digit YoY growth 
in 2027. While the company did mention the risk of decelerating YoY growth for the 
battery industry (which has seen solid YoY growth over the past two years), we expect 
solid growth from smaller segments, including machine tool/general 
machinery/textile/packaging, could offset the risk (we expect the 4 segments to account 
for 24% of total 2027E revenue vs. battery at 13%). 
We believe AirTAC is well positioned to overcome potential industry demand risks with 
its strong B/S and healthy cash flow position, which could further support market share 
gains during industry downturns (historically, AirTAC has accelerated its market share 
gains during industry downcycles, supported by its solid B/S and cash flow 
management). We also expect the company’s ROE to continue to improve in the coming 
years (ROE should improve to 26.2% in 2028E per GSe vs. 16%-17% in 2022-25) 
supporting a high dividend payout ratio (50%-60%+ per year per GSe). 
As a result, we maintain our Buy rating on AirTAC with unchanged 12m TP of NT$1,895.

Earnings and TP revisions

efficiency. We revise down our 2026E EPS estimates by 4% after factoring in a higher tax 
rate in 2H26.

26x on our 2027E EPS, as we believe the company could trade at 0.25x STDV above the 
average PE multiple during the previous pneumatic upcycle.

### Page 6

Investment Thesis - AirTAC
AirTAC is the 2nd-largest pneumatic supplier in mainland China with 28% market share
in 2025, outpacing most local competitors, owing to its low-cost yet high quality
product, and service quality reputation in most key cities in mainland China. We forecast
its market share will reach 30% in 2026E and expect AirTAC to be the structural winner
in a deflationary market, due to its: (1) strong market growth and leadership: AirTAC

continues to expand its market share in mainland China driven by competitive pricing
that is more than 10% lower vs. international peers (with 45%+ GM / 30-35% OPM) and
high demand elasticity among mainland China customers; and (2) superior cost structure
and financial health: with best-in-class cash cost and a solid balance sheet, featuring
positive net cash over FCF since 2023/2024, we believe AirTAC is well-equipped to
weather industry downturns and capitalize on market restructuring.

Overall, we believe AirTAC will see better revenue/profitability in the long term given its
share gainer position in the China pneumatic market and potential to gain share in the
linear guide market (improved demand in the next 1-2 years). This should lead to
strengthened valuation, vs. its recent trading range (trading below its 10-year avg. P/E
multiple). We are Buy rated on AirTAC.
Price target risks and methodology - AirTAC
Valuation methodology: Our 12m TP of NT$1,895 is based on a target P/E of 26.0x on

our 2027E EPS (0.25x STDV above avg of the last pneumatic upcycle).
Key downside risks: (1) Stronger competition from peers; (2) sharper-than-expected
Rmb depreciation, and (3) a longer-than-expected end-market demand downtrend in
mainland China.

on an Investment List is determined by a stock’s total return potential relative to its coverage universe. Any stock not assigned as a Buy or a Sell on an

deemed Neutral. Each region manages Regional Conviction Lists, which are selected from Buy rated stocks on the respective region’s Investment Lists
and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

and represent investment recommendations focused on the size of the total return potential and/or the likelihood of the realization of the return across
their respective areas of coverage.  The addition or removal of stocks from such Conviction Lists are managed by the Investment Review Committee or
other designated committee in each respective region and do not represent a change in the analysts’ investment rating for such stocks.

Total return potential represents the upside or downside differential between the current share price and the price target, including all paid or
anticipated dividends, expected during the time horizon associated with the price target. Price targets are required for all covered stocks. The total
return potential, price target and associated time horizon are stated in each report adding or reiterating an Investment List membership.

研 究  報 告                
聯亞(3081.TT) 
產業：電子–通信網路 
矽光需求持續強勁，更多設備投產與新增後段外包夥伴帶動產能倍增  
 投資評價與建議 
受惠CW Laser出貨量持續增加、新購顯影設備逐步進駐以及後段代工廠產能
開出，預估稅後EPS為21.01/49.3/97.15元，考量NPO和CPO技術逐步成熟且
聯亞具備多波⻑雷射領先技術，從2028年獲利評價，給予40倍本益比。 
 重點評論分析 
矽光產品出貨增加 ， 3Q26營收成⻑ ， 主要客⼾已下⻑期訂單 ， 4Q26營收攀升 ：
3Q26營收季增27.9%至15.6億元 ， 係因矽光產品出貨增加 ， 毛利率因營收規模
擴大上升1.11ppts至58.6%，營業費用較2Q26增加約1,400萬元至1.43億元，
主要來自研發費用以及隨獲利增加按比例提列之員工分紅費用，稅後EPS為
6.12元。4Q26矽光產品需求持續強勁，主要客戶皆已開始下6個⽉以上的⻑期
訂單，訂單能見度佳，隨公司本身與下游代工廠產能逐步開出，預期矽光產品
出貨量逐季成⻑，預估4Q26營收季增23.5%至19.3億元，稅後EPS為7.19元。  
設備與後段代工產能開出， 2027和2028年總產量連續兩年皆年增 150%：展
望2027~2028年，1.6T矽光產品已量產，3.2T與NPO/CPO應用之更高功率矽
光產品則和客戶持續合作開發和驗證中 ， 針對特定客戶之資料中心200G PD已
完成驗證且磊晶產能已規劃，預計未來數⽉將開始量產。目前E-Beam、奈米
壓印技術與DUV深紫外光微影三種技術皆同步進行擴產，隨更多E-Beam投
產，1Q27產能將明顯增加，奈米壓印設備預計於2026年底進廠。 
 投資風險 
設備與後段產能開出進度不如預期。

### Page 2

2

| 營運概況  
矽光產品出貨持續增加與後段產能開出，3Q26營收成⻑，主要客⼾已下⻑期訂單，4Q26營收持續攀升： 3Q26營收季
增27.9%至15.6億元 ， 係因矽光產品出貨量增加 ， 毛利率因營收規模擴大上升1.11ppts至58.6% ， 營業利益為7.71億元，
營業利益率為49.4%，營業費用較2Q26增加約1,400萬元至1.43億元，主要來自研發費用以及隨獲利增加按比例提列之
員工分紅費用，折舊費用較2Q26增加160萬元至7,660萬元，稅後淨利為6.21億元，稅後EPS為6.12元。4Q26矽光產品
需求持續強勁，主要客戶皆已開始下6個⽉以上的⻑期訂單，訂單能見度佳，隨公司本身與下游代工廠產能逐步開出，
預期矽光產品出貨量逐季成⻑，預估4Q26營收季增23.5%至19.3億元，稅後EPS為7.19元。影響毛利率因素有三：整體
營收與產出、匯率變化以及成本。管理層預期營收於接下來幾季將逐季成⻑，惟3Q26前半段台幣升值，隨後9⽉底台幣
轉貶值，匯率變化對3Q26毛利率產生些許不利影響，加上更多人員召募以及設備折舊成本逐步反映，且產品訂價方面，
聯亞僅維持賺取合理利潤，將無法持續大幅漲價，導致毛利率於接下來幾季成⻑幅度將趨緩。 
設備與後段代工產能開出，2027 和 2028 年總產量連續兩年皆年增 150%：展望 2027~2028 年，聯亞 1.6T 矽光產品
已經開始量產，3.2T 與 NPO/CPO 應用之更高功率矽光產品則和客戶持續合作開發和驗證中，另外過去受限產能，公
司設備主要用於生產 LD，自 2027 年開始，針對特定客戶之資料中心 200G 以上規格之 PD 已完成驗證，並已規劃相對
應磊晶產能，將於未來數⽉將開始量產，惟出貨量仍遠小於 LD。2026年 8 ⽉董事會決議與 1 美系資料中心客戶簽訂為
期 4 年之⻑期供貨合約，為滿足該客戶之需求，公司追加約 30 億元資本支出，該資本支出購置之設備可帶動 2027 和
2028 年總產量連續兩年皆年增 150%，另外因應磷化銦基板缺料問題，聯亞目前與 2 家日本主要供應商與 1 家美國主
要供應商達成未來數年的⻑期供貨合約，且美系客戶亦提供部分資金，協助確保後續大規模量產之基板供應無虞。因應
產能不足，聯亞持續新增後段外包合作夥伴位，台灣地區增加數家大型集團作為合作夥伴，該集團在光通訊領域投入意
願高，其資本支出承諾與實力遠高於早期合作夥伴，有助於與聯亞共同爭取國際 AI 訂單。曝光顯影製程方面，目前 E-
Beam、奈米壓印技術與 DUV 深紫外光微影三種技術皆同步進行擴產，首部新購置之 E-Beam 設備已於 2026 年 7 ⽉
開始量產，帶動 7 ⽉營收大幅躍升，隨更多 E-Beam 與 MOCVD 設備投產，1Q27 產能將有明顯增加且未來 8 個季度
產能將逐步增加 ， 奈米壓印設備預計於 2026 年底進場 ， DUV 深紫外光微影設備則於 2027 年以後加入生產 。 綜上所述 ，
預估 2026/2027/2028 年營收年增 155%/130%/86%至56.2/129.1/239.5 億元，稅後 EPS 為 21.01/49.3/97.15 元。

1 
6770 力積電(Powerchip)

報告日期： 2026/10/22 研 究 員：羅  金 鳳 (Sunny Lo)  
壹、訪 談／法 說 內 容 
重點摘要： 
 3Q26 在記憶體及邏輯產品價格季增 20% 的帶動下，使整體毛
利率跳升 19 個百分點 ， 來到47.69% ，單 季 稅 後EPS 1.38 元，
優於預期。  
 4Q26 將反映晶圓投片及產出的時間差，使出貨量及 ASP 將再
增加，帶動營收、毛利率雙位升。  
 AI 帶動矽電容需求大爆發，目前力積電產能僅 1 萬片 /月，但
2027 年需求將達 3～5 萬片 / 月。

一. 反映投片與產出落差， 4Q26 營收、毛利率雙升  
力積電 3Q26 晶圓出貨量 41.6 萬片， QoQ-1.44% ，主因為銅鑼廠
機器搬遷所致，且 3D AI Foundry 營收占比也因遷廠而由 2Q26
的 5.4% 下滑到 1.4%， 但因記憶體及邏輯產品價格持續上漲 ，ASP
季增 20% ，使力積電 3Q26 整體營收達 212.78 億元，
QoQ+23.07% ，YoY+79.71% ，在價格上漲及產能利用率提升的帶
動下，整體毛利率由 2Q26 的 28.28% 大幅上升至 47.69% ，稅後
EPS 1.38 元，優於預期。

由於力積電投片與產出的落差 30%( 邏輯遞延 2～3 個月， DRAM
遞延 4 個月 )，因此， 4Q26 的晶圓出貨及 ASP 將比 3Q26 更高，
加上 ， 銅鑼廠的機器設備已完成搬遷到新竹廠 ，Interposer 及 WoW
的產能將在 11～12 月逐步恢復生產，其中 Interposer 將於 2027
年達到滿載。綜上，我們預估力積電 4Q26 營收 234.3 億元，
QoQ+10.11% ，YoY+87.51% ，毛利率進一步提升至 51.81% ，單
季稅後 EPS 1.64 元，合計 2026 年營收 755.71 億元，
YoY+61.72% ，在記憶體價格大漲的帶動下，毛利率由 2025 年的
-3.29% 跳升至 37.8% ，稅後 EPS 6.58 元(含售廠給美光 )。

二. 2027 年矽電容需求大爆發  
力積電營運轉型將陸續展現成果，美光授權生產的 DRAM 的 1P
製程，將於 2026 完成 PDK ，2Q27 試產， 2028 年有機會貢獻全
年營收； HBM 的後段製程代工 PWF ，由美光提供設備， 2026 年
試產， 4Q27 量產， 2028 年貢獻全年營收； 3D AI Foundry 方面，
堆疊技術將由 Micro bump 進入到 Hybrid bonding ，以及
Interposer 的放量生產 ， 在外溢效果持續下 ，2027 年產能將滿載；
Wafer on Wafer 方面，在銅鑼廠設千完全搬遷至新竹廠後，目前

     11 X 2027 EPS 
參考價位：66.2 元 
     7.67 X 2027 EPS

公司研究報告 
本報告僅供宏遠投顧內部及客戶參考，雖已力求正確與完整，但因時間及市場客觀因素改變所造成產業、市場
或個股之相關條件改變，投資人需自行考量投資之實際狀況與風險，本公司恕不負擔任何法律責任及做任何保
證。非經本公司同意，不得將本報告加以引用或轉載

2 
已小量試產， 1H27 將正式量產。由於建廠及買購設備需大額資本
支出，風險相對較高，因此， PWF 由美光提供設備，未來的 WoW
也將會採取輕資產的模式，降低營運風險，不過，因大廠均將產
能移去生產高階 DRAM ，38nm 、25nm 、20nm 這些傳統 DRAM
製程未來全球將只剩力積電生產，預估此一需求將長達 10～15
年。

此外 ， 因AI 晶片的高功耗及高速傳輸需求 ， 為提升電源傳輸效率 、
降低電阻、去雜訊等問題，將帶動 IPD 需求大爆發，力積電目前
以現有 DRAM 的 38nm、25nm 製程做生產 IPD，恰好目前 DRAM
產能供不應求的情況下 ，IPD 的產能只能配置約 1 萬片 /月，而2027
年全球 IPD 的需求將達 3～5 萬片 /月，且能見度已延伸至 2028
年，顯見需求遠超過力積電所能提供，因此，力積電已在尋找建
廠用地，且將聯合策略客戶共同投資新產能。

三. 預估 2027 年稅後 EPS 8.63 元 
AI 進入到推理後對記憶體已需求量更甚以往，但因全球記憶體產
能嚴重供不應求的情況下， GUP 大廠將 12 層 HBM 降低到 8 層，
顯見記憶體緊俏情況嚴峻，也使記憶體價格易漲難跌，預估 2027
年記憶體價格仍至少維持在高點，加上成熟製程處在有利的價格
環境， 2026 年漲價雙位數後， 2027 年將再漲 10～20% ，因此，
我們預估力積電 2027 年營收 1223.38 億元， YoY+61.89% ，毛利
率 49.89% ，稅後 EPS 8.63 元。

四. 投資建議為買進，目標價 95 元 
受惠 AI 帶動的記憶體漲價潮持續， 2026 年力積電將呈現轉虧為
盈，且 2026 及 2027 年獲利都將大幅成長，加上銅鑼廠售予美光
且變成長期的代工合作夥伴關係，有利力積電長期營運穩定性，
也解決力積電資產負債表壓力，因此，投資建議為買進，目標價
95 元(11X2027 年稅後 EPS) 。

貳、產 業 及 營 運 概 況

一.公司簡介  
力晶集團 05/2019 完成企業重組 ， 由旗下的力晶科技3 座 12 吋晶
圓廠及相關營業、資產，讓與力晶積成電子製造股份有限公司 (簡
稱“力積電” (6770)) 。力積電為聚焦專業晶圓代工、產業定位明
確，目前擁有 2 座 8 吋及 3 座 12 吋晶圓廠，將針對先進記憶體、
客製化邏輯積體電路與分離式元件的三大晶圓代工服務主軸，持
續 Open Foundry 營運模式，從晶片設計、製造服務，到設備、

Company Report 
公司研究報告 
本報告僅供宏遠投顧內部及客戶參考，雖已力求正確與完整，但因時間及市場客觀因素改變所造成產業、市場
或個股之相關條件改變，投資人需自行考量投資之實際狀況與風險，本公司恕不負擔任何法律責任及做任何保
證。非經本公司同意，不得將本報告加以引用或轉載

3 
產能分享，根據不同客戶的屬性和需求，共同建立緊密、彈性的
合作機制，且著眼半導體 3D 堆疊產業大趨勢，力積電與國際知名
大客戶密切配合開發 3D 堆疊封裝，將成為公司長線另一成長動
能。

二.產業趨勢概況  
1、佈局 3D IC 為公司未來運重要利基 
力積電有部份產品屬於記憶體產品，營運雖然將隨著市場波動，
受產業景氣循環大，然力積電善用其技術優勢，將其記憶體產品
與國際知名大廠合作，延伸至 3D 堆疊封裝技術，僅需利用舊製程
即可將晶片效能數倍提升，其以 55nm 的邏輯 IC 堆疊 38nm 的
DRAM 製程，封裝後的晶片用於挖礦機，效能較現有的解決方案
大幅提升 9 倍。由 於 透 過3D 堆疊可使晶片設計效率提高， 提升晶
片整體效能，減少晶片尺寸，加快晶片內部元件溝通速度，為半
導體先進封裝未來重要發展趨勢，且因先進封裝技術成本不斷提
升，客戶為考量使用的成本效益，未來將有更多廠商使用 3D 堆疊
的解決方案取代先進製程技術 ， 使得3D 堆疊技術長線發展具極大
潛力，力積電長線著眼於此一產業大趨勢，將積極深化此一佈局，
為公司長線營運提供良好動能。

2、四大營運方向引領公司未來成長動能 
力積電在經過 2019 年 3 座 8 吋廠及 2 座 12 吋廠的整併，加上邏
輯技術逐漸步上軌道後，使其在 40nm ～0.11um 的技術於全球站
穩腳步，具有指標地位。公司除將以此為基礎拓展多元應用，在
新技術方面亦不斷投入研發，展望未來，擬定四大方向引領公司
成長動能： 1、發展更先進的技術 --- 第四代半導體氧化銦鎵鋅
(IGZO) ，因 IGZO 的材料特性，其超低功耗的性能，僅為目前耗
電的 1/20， 及顯示器解析度能超過5,000DDIP ， 有助應用於AR/VR
的裝置有較佳續航力及超高解析度，使用者將完全沈浸於元宇宙
世界。 2、3D-Interchip 堆疊技術：與晶圓代工大廠台積電合作，
將記憶體與邏輯 IC 進行 3D 堆疊 ， 使晶片具更小體積 、 更低功耗，
更低成本，及開發更有效率。 3D 堆疊的 Wafer on Wafer 目前以
供應挖礦機需求為主，未來將專注於 AI 龐大需求。 3、Interchip ：
將邏輯元件直接放入記憶體元件，以提高記憶體效能，適用於
IoT 、AI 及邊緣運算。 4、車用電子晶片：因應節能減碳的趨勢，
電動車 / 新能源車將快速發展，公司將致力推進第三代半導體
GaN、SiC 技術製程，應用於 PMIC 。由於 PMIC 為車汽應用的重
要零組件之一，且 PMIC 的製造非用最先進的半導體製程，力積
電目前的製程範圍恰好符合最佳生產製程的節點，而電動車的興
起將帶動 PMIC 的應用大幅成長，加上 AI 趨勢確立下，對 PMIC
有更高需求，為公司未來重要佈局方向及營運重要成長動能。

### Page 4

Company Report 
公司研究報告

或個股之相關條件改變，投資人需自行考量投資之實際狀況與風險，本公司恕不負擔任何法律責任及做任何保
證。非經本公司同意，不得將本報告加以引用或轉載

4 
叁、業 績 及 獲 利 預 估

1、預估 4Q26 稅後 EPS 1.64 元 
由於力積電投片與產出的落差 30%( 邏輯遞延 2～3 個月， DRAM
遞延 4 個月 )，因此， 4Q26 的晶圓出貨及 ASP 將比 3Q26 更高，
加上銅鑼廠的機器設備已完成搬遷到新竹廠， Interposer 及 WoW
的產能將在 11～12 月逐步恢復生產，其中 Interposer 將於 2027
年達到滿載。綜上，我們預估力積電 4Q26 營收 234.3 億元，
QoQ+10.11% ，YoY+87.51% ，毛利率進一步提升至 51.81% ，單
季稅後 EPS 1.64 元。

2、預估 2027 年稅後 EPS 8.63 
AI 進入到推理後對記憶體已需求量更甚以往，但因全球記憶體產
能嚴重供不應求的情況下， GUP 大廠將 12 層 HBM 降低到 8 層，
顯見記憶體緊俏情況嚴峻，也使記憶體價格易漲難跌，預估 2027
年記憶體價格仍至少維持在高點，加上成熟製程處在有利的價格
環境， 2026 年漲價雙位數後， 2027 年將再漲 10～20% ，因此，
我們預估力積電 2027 年營收 1223.38 億元， YoY+61.89% ，毛利
率 49.89% ，稅後 EPS 8.63 元。

億豐(8464) 
買進, TP 450 (潛在報酬 25.52%)  
買進 
Price(10/20):358.5 
Target Price：450

隨客製化需求持續成長、柬埔寨廠人力恢復至5,000人並開始協助
客戶回補庫存，帶動3Q26營收創下歷史新高，且受惠於營收規模
擴大、產能利用率提升及關稅返還挹注，淨利率有望創高。展望後
市，公司持續擴增墨西哥產能，員工人數已達1,800人，後續預計
每年增加約 500 人，以改善交期並滿足客製化窗簾訂單成長需求；
越南新廠預計 2027年初投產，第一期規劃員工約 1,000人，可望
進一步擴大客製化產品產能與市佔率。市場需求方面，儘管美國市
場面臨高通膨及消費降級壓力，但公司憑藉穩健的財務結構，客製
化產品與同業價差已擴大至 30%～40%，具價格競爭優勢，有助
於提升市佔率及整體訂單量，預期維持 mid-single digit成長；固
定尺寸則透過新品推出，支撐營收維持 low-single digit成長；歐
洲市場方面，隨荷蘭、比利時及丹麥等地的覆蓋率提升，可望延續
high-single digit 成長。獲利方面，受惠於關稅稅率降至 20%、
漲價及營收規模效益逐步顯現，預期毛利率將延續成長趨勢。綜合
以上，研究部預估 2026、2027 年 EPS 為 23.92、27.91 元，以
16倍本益比評價，給予目標價 450元，投資建議為買進。  
 近期投資評等  2Q26 毛利率創高，惟油價及運價上漲壓抑獲利表現 
2Q26 營收 79.27 億元，YoY+2.86%，QoQ+8.52%，稅後損
益 13.91 億元，YoY+20.67%，QoQ-15.57%，EPS 4.75 元，
低於研究部預期。第二季為傳統消費旺季，營運表現較第一季
明顯回升。區域營收方面，北美市場受到消費降級影響，加上
柬埔寨廠人力短缺，營收YoY-2.1%；非美市場則受惠於荷蘭、
比利時及丹麥等地的覆蓋率提升，營收 YoY+19.8%，成為主
要成長動能。獲利方面，2Q26 毛利率 59.95%，YoY+0.7ppt，
QoQ+0.9ppt，受惠於客制化及歐洲市場營收比重持續提升，
帶動毛利率創歷史新高，惟油價及燃油附加費急漲，使費用率
升至 32.7%，抵銷部分毛利率改善效益，壓抑整體獲利表現。

 柬埔寨廠產能恢復，固定尺寸產品開始回補庫存 
展望 3Q26，9M26 營收 26.62 億元 ，YoY+13.5%，MoM-5.5%，
累計前 9 月營收 237.14 億元，YoY+6.3%，優於研究部預期。
第三季營收創歷史新高，台幣營收 YoY+16%、美金營收
YoY+9%，主要受惠於客制化需求持續成長，且柬埔寨廠已補
足至 5,000 人，開始協助客戶補庫存。獲利方面，隨營收規模
擴大、產能利用率提升，加上關稅返還挹注，預估3Q26 毛利

現金股利(去年) 14.5 率將優於去年同期；儘管油價及燃油附加費仍高於去年同期，
但較第二季小幅回落，加上營收規模擴大有助於費用率下降，
獲利表現可望改善。綜合以上， 3Q26 營收 84.82 億元，
YoY+15.92% ， QoQ+6.99% ， 稅 後 損 益19.53 億 元 ，
YoY+14.44%，QoQ+40.42%，EPS 6.67 元。

 越南新廠預計 1Q27投產，挹注未來 3~4年營運成長 
公司客製化窗簾產能主要布局於中國及墨西哥，分別占整體產
能約 60%及 40%。中國廠員工人數約 4,000~4,500 人；墨西
哥則鎖定北美市場，截至 2026 年 9 月，舊廠與新廠合計員工
已達 1,800 人，預計逐年增加約 500 名員工，以改善交期問
題並因應市佔擴張需求，透過規模經濟及生產效率提升，進一
步優化整體獲利結構。柬埔寨廠則以固定尺寸窗簾為主，1H26
受缺工影響，出貨進度放緩， 6M26 起員工人數已由先前的
4,000 人回升至 5,000人，並開始協助客戶回補庫存至健康水
位。越南新廠預計於 2027 年初投產，聚焦客制化產品，第一
期規劃員工人數約 1,000 人，為中長期成長動能。

 同業價差擴大提升競爭力，歐洲市場成長動能延續 
儘管美國消費市場面臨高通膨、消費降級及關稅等壓力，但公
司憑藉穩健的財務結構，與同業的價差已擴大至30%～40%，
加上產品持續迭代更新，有助於提升市佔率及整體訂單量，預
估 2026~2027 年客製化業務可維持 mid-single digit成長；固
定尺寸則透過新品免打孔窗廉推出，提高平均售價，以維持
low-single digit 成長。歐洲市場方面，採線上直營電商模式，
專業人員到府量測與安裝服務，由於服務利潤率較高，已成為
主要獲利來源。目前已拓展至荷蘭、比利時、丹麥等市場，隨
覆蓋率快速提升，預期非美地區將延續 high-single digit 成
長。電動窗簾方面，價格為手動款的 2～2.5 倍，儘管目前佔
比仍為 low-single digit，但 CAGR 達 15%～20%以上，預期
未來營收占比有望提升至 10%，進一步帶動整體 ASP 及產品
組合優化。

 漲價效益與產品組合優化，支撐毛利率續創高 
原料成本方面，受美伊戰爭反覆升溫影響，PVC 價格維持 850
美元/噸的相對高檔，然公司近年持續調整配方並提高環保材
料使用比重，降低對 PVC 價格波動的依賴，以減輕短期原料

3
成本變動對獲利的衝擊。此外，公司已於 2M26 啟動漲價計
劃，有助於緩解成本上升壓力，加上客製化產品營收比重逐步
擴大、關稅稅率降至 20%，預期全年毛利率仍將維持成長趨
勢。綜合以上，研究部預估  2026 年營收 322.33 億元，
YoY+7.56%，稅後損益 70.1 億元，YoY+6.19%，EPS 23.92
元；2027 年營收 351.08億元，YoY+8.92%，稅後損益 81.77
億元，YoY+16.65%，EPS 27.91 元。

 結論與建議 
隨客製化需求持續成長、柬埔寨廠人力恢復至5,000人並開始
協助客戶回補庫存，帶動3Q26營收創下歷史新高，且受惠於
營收規模擴大、產能利用率提升及關稅返還挹注，淨利率有望
創高。展望後市，公司持續擴增墨西哥產能，員工人數已達
1,800人，後續預計每年增加約500人，以改善交期並滿足客製
化窗簾訂單成長需求；越南新廠預計2027年初投產，第一期規
劃員工約1,000人，可望進一步擴大客製化產品產能與市佔
率。市場需求方面，儘管美國市場面臨高通膨及消費降級壓
力，但公司憑藉穩健的財務結構，客製化產品與同業價差已擴
大至30%～40%，具價格競爭優勢，有助於提升市佔率及整體
訂單量，預期維持mid-single digit成長；固定尺寸則透過新品
推出，支撐營收維持low-single digit成長；歐洲市場方面，隨
荷蘭、比利時及丹麥等地的覆蓋率提升，可望延續high-single 
digit成長。獲利方面，受惠於關稅稅率降至20%、漲價及營收
規模效益逐步顯現，預期毛利率將延續成長趨勢。綜合以上，
研究部預估2026、2027年EPS為23.92、27.91元，以16倍本
益比評價，給予目標價450元，投資建議為買進。

## Document 278: [402340] SK스퀘어(402340) 하이닉스 자사주 소각 이후가 더 기대SK스퀘어(402340) 하이닉스 자사주 소각 이후가 더 기대하이닉스 자사주 소각_31fbe439

Source PDF: all report pdf/[402340] SK스퀘어(402340) 하이닉스 자사주 소각 이후가 더 기대SK스퀘어(402340) 하이닉스 자사주 소각 이후가 더 기대하이닉스 자사주 소각_31fbe439.pdf
Report source: Hankyung
Date: 2026-09-30
Pages: 6
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

SK 스퀘어 (402340/KS) 
하이닉스 자사주 소각 이후가 더 기대 
예정보다 빠른 SK 하이닉스 자사주 매입 
SK스퀘어의 자회사인 SK하이닉스는 지난 8월 20일부터 11월 19일까지 자사주 40
조원(2,407만주, 3.3%)을 매입 예정이다. 9월 29일까지 1,715만주, 예정 매입수량 
대비 71.3%를 매입 완료하였다. 매입 초기 매일 65만주 매입하다가 9월 10일부터는 
60만주를 매입하고 있는데, 이러한 추세가 유지된다면 10월 중순경 자사주 매입이 마
무리될 예정이다. 자사주 매입 후 소각 예정으로 소각이 완료되면 SK스퀘어의 SK하아
닉스에 대한 지분율은 20.0%에서 20.7%로 상승하게 된다.

자사주 매입 이후 추가적인 주주환원에 대한 기대 
SK하이닉스는 지난 8월 19일 정책기간(‘25년~’27년) 동안 발생하는 누적 FCF의 
50% 이상을 주주환원 하는 주주환원 정책을 발표한 바 있다. 26년 SK하이닉스의 예
상 FCF는 164조원(컨센서스 기준)으로 50%를 주주환원 할 경우 자사주 매입 금액
(40 조원)을 제외한 주주환원 재원은 42 조원이다. 잔여 주주환원 재원이 모두 배당으
로 지급된다면 SK 하이닉스 자사주 소각 이후 SK 스퀘어의 지분율은 20.7%이므로 
SK스퀘어의 배당수입도 8.7조원에 달한다. 지난 3월 SK스퀘어는 ‘26년~’28년 경
상배당수입의 30% 이상을 주주에게 환원하는 정책을 발표했다. 보수적으로 30%만 
가정하더라도 SK스퀘어의 배당재원은 2.6조원(현 시가총액 대비 1.8%)이다. SK스퀘
어는 연초 주주총회에서 배당가능이익 확보 차원에서 자본준비금 5 조 8,900억원을 
이익잉여금으로 전입한 바 있다. 이는 26년 재무제표가 확정되는 27년 3월 이후 배당
가능이익으로 편입되므로 SK스퀘어의 주주환원 확대가 기대된다.

투자의견 매수, 목표주가 185 만원(유지) 
SK스퀘어의 순자산 가치 중 98% 이상이 SK하이닉스 지분가치이다. SK하이닉스는 
자사주 매입 이후 17.7% 주가가 상승했으나, SK 스퀘어 주가는 11.4% 상승했다. SK
하이닉스 자사주 매입·소각은 SK 스퀘어의 보유 지분가치 상승뿐 아니라 지분율도 상
승하기 때문에 자사주가 소각될 경우 SK스퀘어의 주가가 탄력적으로 움직일 가능성이 
높다. SK스퀘어 현재 NAV 대비 할인율은 43.7%, 12MF PBR 1.4배로 7월 이후 주
가 조정으로 밸류에이션 매력도 높아졌다. 투자의견 매수와 목표주가 185만원을 유지
한다.

## Document 291: AI资本开支见顶？高盛：拐点近了，AI正从“超级主题”变成“常规叙事”

Source PDF: all report pdf/AI资本开支见顶？高盛：拐点近了，AI正从“超级主题”变成“常规叙事”.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-02
Pages: 5
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

⾼盛判断， AI 资本开⽀的峰值增速已近在眼前，这⼀主题正从持续三年的 “ 超级充电叙事 ” ，
变成好坏两⾯都要算的 “ 常规故事 ” ，市场对其的定价已不再有单边⽀撑。与此同时，标普
500 和纳斯达克过去六个⽉基本原地踏步，本轮财报季 “ ⾜以守住阵线，但不⾜以再次点燃
突破⾏情 ” 。
摘要：
持续三年的 AI 超级周期叙事，或许正在悄然切换档位。⾼盛三位资深⼈⼠在最新⼀期播客对话中
判断： AI 资本开⽀的峰值增速已近在眼前，市场对 AI 主题的定价，已经从 “ 只往好处想 ” 的单边乐
观转向 “ 好坏两⾯都要算 ” 的双⾯博弈。⽽与此同时，美联储的实际加息幅度很可能低于市场当前
的定价预期。
录制于 10 ⽉ 29 ⽇的这期《 The Breaks of the Game 》节⽬中，⾼盛全球对冲基⾦覆盖主管 Tony
Pasquariello 、全球⻛险主管 Josh Schiffrin ，以及⾼盛研究部⾼级顾问 Dominic Wilson ，在超级
科技股财报周刚过之际，对 AI 资本开⽀叙事的演变、美联储路径及宏观⻛险墙进⾏了系统性复

盘。 Dominic Wilson 直⾔，AI 已从⼀个 “ 超级充电主题 ” 过渡到 “ 更常规的叙事 ” ，增⻓率的峰值
“ ⼤概率已相当接近 ” 。
这⼀判断的背景是：标普 500 指数在过去六个⽉基本原地踏步，纳斯达克同样如此。 Tony
Pasquariello 指出，本轮财报季 “ 表⾯上还不错 ” ，但前瞻盈利的上修幅度远不及此前 Q1 、 Q2 时
期，“ ⾜以守住阵线，但不⾜以再次点燃突破⾏情 ” 。
与此同时， Josh Schiffrin 将中东局势升级列为当前最⼤的近期增⻓⻛险，并将“ 做多全球前端利
率 ”列为年底最⾼确信度交易。
AI 从 “ 超级主题 ” 变成 “ 双向故事 ”
当⼀个主题被市场按 “ 最乐观路径 ” 定价时，只要出现⼀点不及预期的信息，负⾯冲击就会被放
⼤。⾼盛的判断是， AI 基础设施投资正处于⼀个临界点。
AI 交易⾛到这⼀步，是三条线索同时出现的结果。第⼀，市场价格已经包含了很乐观的预期；第
⼆，投资增速的峰值临近；第三，为这轮投资融资的信贷需求，开始在市场上留下痕迹。三条

叠在⼀起，这个主题就从 “ 信就⾏ ” 变成了 “ 得算账 ”—— 光靠信念不够，得看融资、看盈利、看
利率能不能撑住。
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

Dominic Wilson 在节⽬中拆解指出，⾃去年 6 ⽉ AI 相关板块出现那轮 “ 泡沫式峰值 ” 以来，该⾏⼀
直担忧市场对整个 AI 空间的定价已经过⾼ ——“ 不是说定价离谱，⽽是处于⼀个需要相当乐观的
路径才能⽀撑当前价格的⽔平，尤其是在基础设施领域 ” 。
⽽超级科技股财报季提供的数据，已不再是⼀边倒的信息。 Wilson 的原话是：“ 你在盈利端看到
的是⼀个散点图。我们⾃⼰的预测显⽰，投资冲动的峰值⼤概不会太远了。增⻓还会有，但峰
值增速⼤概率已相当接近。 ”
他进⼀步列举了三个具体的裂缝，包括融资和变现的疑问：其⼀，超级科技公司的信⽤融资需
求带来的信⽤利差压⼒ “ 时隐时现，但确实存在⼀定的跑输 ” ；其⼆，超级科技公司的变现路径上
“ 仍有问号 ” ；其三，盈利端的表现出现了分化，⽽⾮此前的整⻬划⼀。不过， Dominic 谈的是增
速峰值，没有说资本开⽀的绝对规模会下降。
Wilson 的结论是：“ ⼀旦你⾯对的是这种局⾯ —— 主要故事的增⻓率⻅顶、利率上⾏、信⽤利差

⾛阔 —— 感觉就像是⼀个更常规的叙事。它曾经是⼀个超级充电主题，持续了⼤概三年。现在
⻛险的天平更加两⾯，故事也更加常规。 ”
财报盈利托住了指数，却推不动指数
过去半年，美股的运⾏逻辑是⼀场拉锯：利率、能源以及⼀定程度上的信贷构成逆⻛，企业盈
利负责托底。结果是指数没有净位移。盈利季的问题不在于差，⽽在于 “ 刚好够⽤ ” ：业绩过了
关，但上调预期的幅度不⾜以让市场重新突破。
这是市场此刻最焦虑的地⽅。⼆季度财报期间，投资者曾意识到今年可能是盈利增⻓异常强劲
的⼀年，预期随之⼤幅上修。本轮财报没有重现这种效应。 Tony 说：“ 标普基本⼜回到了 7000
点 …… 纳斯达克这半年也没⾛出什么⽅向。 ”

### Page 3

“ 就这⼀季来看，盈利表⾯上还不错。⻔槛很⾼ …… 我觉得最有意思的是，前瞻盈利没有像⼀季
度、⼆季度财报期那样被上调。 ”
在他看来，全年盈利增⻓最终会很可观，可能达到接近 30% 甚⾄更⾼（按其所述⼝径，实录此
处⽂字记录不够清晰，发稿前建议核对⾳频）。但今年的数字已基本被市场消化。 Tony 的概括
是：“ 好到⾜以守住阵线，但还不⾜以添燃料，让市场再来⼀次突破。 ”
美联储：市场定价了太多，实际加息或⽌步于此
在 AI 叙事降温的同时，利率路径的不确定性构成了另⼀条主线。 Josh Schiffrin 对美联储 10 ⽉会
议的解读整体偏鸽，并明确认为市场当前的加息定价存在⾼估。
Schiffrin 的判断是：美联储⽬前认为⾃⼰有能⼒ “ 有条不紊地⾏动 ” 。尽管通胀形势依然严峻，但 9
⽉的政策⾏动已在产⽣效果，⾦融条件虽未收紧⾄极端⽔平，但已有所收紧。他的基准情景
是：12 ⽉再加息⼀次，此后或许再加⼀次，但 “ 最终的加息次数很可能少于市场当前的预期 ” 。
他的原话是： “ 你现在⼤约有 60 到 65 个基点的进⼀步加息被定价进去。我确实认为他们会在 12 ⽉

加息，但那很可能就是全部了。也许再多⼀次。但我的基准情景，就像我们上个⽉讨论的，是
12 ⽉再加⼀次。 ”
Schiffrin 同时指出，美联储从 2027 、 2028 年起将减少政策声明的频率，从每年⼋次会议缩减⾄
七次，但他认为这不会对市场产⽣重⼤影响： “ 有些会议本来就相隔很近，两次会议之间发⽣的
事情也不多，所以我不认为这会对市场产⽣很⼤影响。 ”

### Page 4

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。
Dominic Wilson 在利率问题上与 Schiffrin 的判断⾼度⼀致。他指出，此前利率上⾏的部分原因在
于， 10 年期远期 OIS 利率⼀度触及 5.20% 左右 ——“ 这已经⾮常接近过去 20 年政策利率周期的峰
值，如果你把这个作为 10 年后的中⼼点，那你已经建⽴了相当多的⻛险溢价 ” 。他认为， AI 叙事
的动摇也在通过 “ 增⻓担忧 ” 的渠道压低了收益率。
宏观⻛险墙：最⼤威胁来⾃中东，最被低估的是油价下⾏
在宏观⻛险的排序上，三位对话者的判断出现了⼀个有趣的分歧与互补： Josh Schiffrin 将中东
局势升级列为最⼤的近期⻛险，⽽ Dominic Wilson 则认为，油价 “ 不升级 ” 的尾部⻛险反⽽是当前
最被市场低估的机会。
Schiffrin 的逻辑直接：⼀旦冲突升级、能源价格再度上涨，这是最⼤的增⻓威胁；反之，若局势

缓和、油价回落， “ 这对资产整体有利，⽆论是股票还是债券 ” 。
Wilson 则从另⼀个⻆度切⼊。他认为，市场已经为伊朗地缘政治⻛险建⽴了相当多的⻛险溢
价，油市中存在真实的⻛险溢价。因此， “ 我们只是不跟进，或者升级程度不及市场担忧的那个
部分，我认为是被低估的 ” 。他的具体情景是：如果中期选举过后两三周内没有出现重⼤升级，
“ 市场会发现这⽐它所担忧的路径要好，因为我们已经建⽴了这种预期。即便没有任何实质性进
展，仅仅是没有新的坏消息，就已经⾜够了 ” 。
在年底最⾼确信度交易上， Schiffrin 明确指向“ 全球前端利率做多 ”—— 覆盖美国、欧洲和英国，
理由是各主要央⾏的实际⾏动将少于市场定价，⽽信⽤市场⼀旦出现波动，也会为前端利率提
供下⾏通道。
Tony Pasquariello 则对整体市场格局做出了总结性描述：过去六个⽉，宏观层⾯的利率、能源
和信⽤构成逆⻛，⽽盈利则 “ 守住了阵线 ” 。标普 500 和纳斯达克基本在原地踏步。本轮财报季 “ 表
⾯上还不错 ” ，但前瞻盈利的上修⼒度远不及 Q1 、 Q2 时期， “ ⾜以守住阵线，但不⾜以再次点燃

突破⾏情 ” 。他的结论是：如果 Schiffrin 和 Wilson 的判断正确，年底前股市或许能⾛出⼀段 “ 不错
的滑⾏路径 ” ，但可能是 “ 不同类型的股票 ” 在领涨。

## Document 305: A股情绪见底了吗？

Source PDF: all report pdf/A股情绪见底了吗？.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-11-02
Pages: 5
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

中信证券认为，情绪指数于 10 ⽉ 30 ⽇跌破 10 进⼊恐慌区， A 股正式开启新⼀轮探底⾏情。
当前换⼿率仍在 1% 上⽅、融资买⼊降⾄⽜市以来最低、股债收益差创 2016 年以来最弱 ——
多项指标显⽰情绪尚未⻅底。后市或呈 “V 型底 ” 急跌抄底或 “U 型底 ” 漫⻓筑底两种演绎，救
市流动性⽀撑成关键变量。
摘要：
核⼼观点
情绪指数于 10 ⽉ 14 ⽇跌破 25 进⼊低迷区，标志着市场从震荡市转为熊市。 10 ⽉ 30 ⽇情绪指数再
次跌破 10 进⼊恐慌区，标志着 A 股将进⼊新⼀轮探底⾏情。市场陷⼊恐慌区之初可能由于被动去
杠杆或类似被动卖盘的担忧⽽出现恐慌性抛售和加速下跌的情况。要警惕市场陷⼊流动性危机
⽽出现负反馈问题，救市资⾦提供的流动性⽀持⾄关重要。当前情绪指数尚未⻅底，后续若情
绪指数⼤幅下滑并达到情绪冰点，则意味着市场到达 “V 型底 ” 底部，在最恐慌时刻将迎来最佳抄
底时机（如 24 年 1 ⽉）；若情绪指数在恐慌区窄幅震荡但未跌⾄情绪冰点，意味着市场仍将缓慢

向下探寻 “U 型底 ” 底部，需要耐⼼等待情绪指数回升⾄ 10 以上脱离恐慌区才能迎来做多机遇（如
24 年 6-9 ⽉）。
A 股情绪⻅底了吗？
换⼿率：在经历连续 3 个⽉缩量后，量能接近本轮⽜市以来最低⽔平。但当前换⼿率仍处于 1%
上⽅，意味着市场尚未充分缩量，后续存在⼆次探底⻛险。
偏股型基⾦新发量： 10 ⽉末偏股型基⾦新发量⼤幅上升⾄近年来偏⾼的⽔平，主要源⾃主动
ETF 基⾦集中发⾏的影响，剔除这⼀影响后，该指标仍处于低位。
融资买⼊占⽐：融资买⼊占⽐指标 10 ⽉初⼤幅下滑，⽉中已经降⾄本轮⽜市以来最低⽔平，下
旬窄幅震荡未能明显回升，显⽰杠杆资⾦情绪悲观。
隐含⻛险溢价：指标 10 ⽉延续上⾏趋势，从⽉初 3.08% 快速升⾄⽉末 3.51% ，显⽰权益资产性价
⽐显著提升。
股债收益差：指标 9 ⽉初快速下滑转负后基本都处于零轴下⽅， 10 ⽉末降⾄ -13.78% ，市场亏钱
效应为  2016 年  3 ⽉以来最强。
超 60MA ：指标 10 ⽉下滑后下旬已经跌破  20% 警戒线，反映市场资⾦⾯持续恶化，使得市场陷

⼊异常弱势状态（类似  201 8 年、 2024 年）。
超买超卖：8 ⽉以来⻓期处于零轴附近下⽅位置， 10 ⽉末加速下⾏跌⾄ -12.5% 附近，揭⽰当前市
场仍处于弱势震荡状态，短期可能出现技术性反弹，但⼤的超跌反弹动能仍然偏弱。
正⽂
2022 年 3 ⽉底，我们推出了中信建投策略 - 投资者情绪指数，由多个市场公开交易指标合成⽽
来。该指数在 A 股历史⾏情重要区间都较好地反映了当时市场情绪⽔平，并且极⾼和极低点能够
领先于⾏情反转，具有⼀定的预测能⼒。需要指出的是，该指数⽤于刻画市场的投资者情绪，
是同步指标，其预测性主要由投资者情绪对市场的预测性来体现。投资者情绪指数推出后，受
到了⼤量投资者的关注，因此我们从 2022 年 4 ⽉底开始，以⽉报的形式，对当前市场情绪进⾏跟
⾸⻚ 资讯 快讯 ⾏情 ⽇历 APP VIP 会员 ⼤师课 ⻅闻历 搜索

### Page 2

踪和展⽰，并给出分项核⼼情绪指标的历史⾛势和最新动向。 2024 年 8 ⽉，我们再度推出了专题
报告，回顾了情绪指数发布近两年半时间市场择时的实战效果，并总结了不同情绪状态下不同
市场⻛格的表现。
A 股情绪⻅底了吗？
10 ⽉投资者情绪指数并未根据季节效应规律在国庆节后出现回升，反⽽⼤幅下滑，这提⽰了 A 股
市场情绪异常快速下⾏和增量资⾦明显缺乏的问题。最终情绪指数于 10 ⽉ 14 ⽇跌破 25 进⼊低迷
区，结合万得全 A 指数⼤幅低于年线下⽅，标志着市场从震荡市转为熊市。 10 ⽉ 30 ⽇情绪指数再
次跌破 10 进⼊恐慌区，标志着 A 股将进⼊新⼀轮探底⾏情。
当情绪指数跌⼊恐慌区后如何应对？根据历史经验，市场陷⼊恐慌区之初可能由于被动去杠杆
或类似被动卖盘（险资、固收 + 等）的担忧⽽出现恐慌性抛售和加速下跌的情况。要警惕市场陷
⼊流动性危机⽽出现负反馈问题，救市资⾦提供的流动性⽀持⾄关重要。当前情绪指数尚未⻅
底，后续如果情绪指数⼤幅下滑并达到情绪冰点，则意味着市场到达 “V 型底 ” 的底部，在最恐慌

时刻将迎来最佳抄底时机（例如 2024 年 1 ⽉）；如果情绪指数在恐慌区窄幅震荡但未跌⾄情绪冰
点，意味着市场仍将缓慢向下探寻 “U 型底 ” 的底部，需要耐⼼等待情绪指数回升⾄ 10 以上脱离恐
慌区才能迎来做多机遇（例如 2024 年 6-9 ⽉）。
我们对各分项指标进⾏分析。七⼤指标中，换⼿率、偏股基⾦发⾏量、融资买⼊占⽐、隐含⻛
险溢价、股债收益差数据经过 5 ⽇平均平滑；超 60MA 、超买超卖两项指标原为周度数据，为了
更加灵敏现在统⼀进⾏⽇度化， 60 周均线实质上改成了 300 ⽇均线。下⽂均默认使⽤这⼀⼝径。

### Page 3

换⼿率：10 ⽉换⼿率低位震荡，但仍处于 1% 上⽅。在经历连续 3 个⽉缩量后，量能接近本轮⽜
市以来最低⽔平。但当前换⼿率仍处于 1% 上⽅，意味着市场尚未充分缩量，后续存在⼆次探底
⻛险。
偏股型基⾦新发量：剔除主动 ETF 基⾦集中发⾏影响后，偏股型基⾦新发量仍处于低位。 10 ⽉
末偏股型基⾦新发量⼤幅上升⾄近年来偏⾼的⽔平，主要源⾃主动 ETF 基⾦集中发⾏的影响，剔
除这⼀影响后，该指标仍处于低位。
融资买⼊占⽐：10 ⽉低位下滑，⽉中降⾄本轮⽜市以来最低⽔平。融资买⼊占⽐指标 10 ⽉初⼤
幅下滑，⽉中已经降⾄本轮⽜市以来最低⽔平，下旬窄幅震荡未能明显回升，显⽰杠杆资⾦情
绪悲观。

### Page 4

隐含⻛险溢价：10 ⽉⼤幅上⾏，权益资产性价⽐显著提升。指标 10 ⽉延续上⾏趋势，从⽉初
3.08% 快速升⾄⽉末 3.51% ，显⽰权益资产性价⽐显著提升。
股债收益差：10 ⽉⼤幅下⾏，⽉末降⾄ 2016 年 3 ⽉以来最低⽔平，市场亏钱效应显著。指标 9 ⽉
初快速下滑转负后基本都处于零轴下⽅， 10 ⽉末降⾄ -13.78% ，市场亏钱效应为 2016 年 3 ⽉以来
最强。
超 60MA ：10 ⽉低位下滑，下旬已经跌破 20% 警戒线，反映市场资⾦⾯持续恶化。该指标以中⻓
期视⻆刻画市场强弱状况，反映市场上收盘价在 60 周均线（ 300 ⽇均线）上⽅的个股⽐例。从历
史上看该指标超过 80%/ 低于 20% 往往意味着市场情绪的过热 / 过冷，⾏情有出现反转的可能。但
指标 10 ⽉下滑后下旬已经跌破 20% 警戒线，反映市场资⾦⾯持续恶化，使得市场陷⼊异常弱势
状态（类似 2018 年、 2024 年）。

### Page 5

⻛险提⽰及免责条款
市场有⻛险，投资需谨慎。本⽂不构成个⼈投资建议，也未考虑到个别⽤⼾特殊的投资⽬标、财务状况或需要。⽤⼾应考虑本⽂
中的任何意⻅、观点或结论是否符合其特定状况。据此投资，责任⾃负。

期视⻆刻画市场强弱情况，揭⽰当前市场仍处于弱势震荡状态，短期可能出现技术性反弹，但
⼤的超跌反弹动能仍然偏弱。

Top 5 ngành tăng / giảm  VNINDEX giảm điểm trong tuần qua, -49.02 điểm tương ứng -2.66%, với thanh khoản cải 
thiện. Dệt may, Y tế và Bất động sản là các nhóm ngành có mức giảm rõ rệt nhất trong 
tuần. Khối ngoại duy trì bán ròng so với xu hướng của tuần trước với giá trị -4,944 tỷ đồng, 
tập trung ở các mã VPB, TCB và VHM; mặt khác, tự doanh đảo chiều mua ròng ở mức 1995 
tỷ đồng.

Ở một khía cạnh khác, khối ngoại bán ròng -5,280 HĐTL VN30F1M trong tuần qua và đang 
duy trì vị thế bán lũy kế 3,630 hợp đồng. Tự doanh mua ròng 208 hợp đồng nhưng đang 
duy trì trạng thái lũy kế bán ròng -6,428 hợp đồng.

TTCK Việt Nam tiếp tục điều chỉnh tuần thứ hai liên tiếp sau nâng hạng, khi VN-Index lùi 
về đóng cửa 1.737 điểm — giảm khoảng 2% so với tuần trước và đang kiểm định lại vùng 
đáy cũ của tháng 8, quanh 1.720. Áp lực bán duy trì khi chỉ số giảm nhiều phiên liên tiếp, 
cho thấy hiệu ứng "mua kỳ vọng, bán thực tế" sau nâng hạng vẫn chưa kết thúc, trong bối 
cảnh dòng tiền thận trọng và thanh khoản ở mức thấp. Khối ngoại tiếp tục là lực cản 
chính khi bán ròng mạnh cả 5 phiên trong tuần, riêng phiên 2/10 bán ròng tới 3.361 tỷ 
đồng, tập trung ở PNJ và HDB. Áp lực bên ngoài vẫn hiện diện khi Fed duy trì lập trường 
diều hâu và giá dầu ở mức cao; trong một, hai tuần tới, mùa báo cáo KQKD sẽ bắt đầu có 
tác động, kỳ vọng theo chiều hướng tích cực, qua đó giúp nâng đỡ thị trường chung.

Ngưỡng kỹ thuật

VNINDEX hình thành cây nến giảm điểm tiêu cực trên khung tuần, xuyên thủng vùng hỗ trợ 
gần quanh 1750, cho thấy áp lực bán đã có phần quyết liệt hơn sau 2 tuần giằng co trước 
đó. Trên khung ngày, sau các phiên lình xình vào đầu tuần, chỉ số cũng dần chuyển sang 
diễn biến giảm điểm rõ nét hơn về cuối tuần, đồng thời cắt sâu xuống dưới SMA50, thể 
hiện áp lực phân phối đang có phần lấn át mặc dù chưa xảy ra hiện tượng bán tháo khi 
khối lượng khớp lệnh được giữ ở mức thấp. Diễn biến tính từ vùng đỉnh 1870 vào đầu tháng 
9 đang không mấy khả quan, tuy nhiên xu hướng tăng ngắn hạn, tính từ đáy 1640 vào cuối 
tháng 7, hiện vẫn đang được bảo lưu và VNINDEX được kỳ vọng sẽ có nhịp hồi phục trở lại 
khi về đến vùng hỗ trợ kế tiếp tại quanh 1710.

Danh mục đầu tư mẫu

Phương pháp tiếp cận: 
- Danh mục tập hợp các cổ phiếu tiềm năng nhất được KBSV 
lựa chọn và tái cơ cấu theo tháng. 
- Ngưỡng cắt lỗ cần được cân nhắc cho mỗi mã là -15%. 
- Danh mục giả định luôn duy trì tỷ trọng 100% là cổ phiếu với 
trọng số được phân bổ đều giữa các mã.

Lưu ý:  
- NĐT được khuyến nghị mua tại mức giá bằng hoặc thấp hơn 
giá khuyến nghị mua. 
- Lợi nhuận kỳ vọng được tính theo giá khuyến nghị mua. 
 So sánh hiệu suất với VN30 Index

## Document 340: Downgrade of global equities to a Core allocation

Source PDF: all report pdf/Downgrade of global equities to a Core allocation.pdf
Report source: 渣打銀行 (Market Watch)
Date: 2026-10-30
Pages: 9
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

Downgrade of global 
equities to a Core allocation  
Summary 
Event: We are downgrading global equities from Overweight 
to a Neutral/Core allocation. We continue to maintain a 
Neutral allocation to fixed income, Overweight on Gold and 
Underweight on Cash. There are more details in our Global 
Market Outlook publication on 30 October 2026. 
What does the equity downgrade mean?  
This downgrade implies a slightly lower allocation to global 
equities within a diversified portfolio. For our CIO Foundation 
Balanced portfolio, this would imply an equity allocation of 
52.5%, down from 53 .2%. We continue to expect positive 
returns for global equities  in the coming 6 -12 months , 
though equity investors may need to recalibrate expectations 
lower, compared with the 15 -20% annualised gains 
experienced in the last few years. Our capital market 
assumptions still suggest a mid -to-high single digit annual 
returns over the next few years, consistent with a transition 
to a mid -cycle bull market . Consensus estimates point to 
16% earnings growth for global equities in 2027, which 
should overcome various headwinds to deliver positive 
returns for equities over the next 12 months. Investors should 
thus, continue to maintain exposure to equities  within a 
diversified portfolio. 
In addition, we see a changing playbook  to prepare for 
higher volatility, more active management and greater 
diversification, as discussed below. 
What does it mean for regional equities 
positioning? 
Within a global equities portfolio, we remain Overweight US 
and Asia ex -Japan (AxJ) equities , which are expected to 
deliver the highest earnings growth in 2027 amongst the 
major regions. This growth is led by the technology sector, 
which carries the largest weight in both regions. Within the 
diversified equities portfolio, we maintain Core allocations to

Allocation to global equities decrease to 52.5% from 
53.2% for our CIO Foundation Balanced portfolio 
Foundation tactical asset allocation

Source: Standard Chartered

next 12m, as of 29 October 2026

### Page 2

Standard Chartered Bank 
WS Global CIO Office | 30 October 2026

Euro area equities, which offer lower valuations, and Japan 
equities, which is backed by a reflating economy. We are 
Underweight UK equities, where the sector composition 
offers lower growth prospects in 2027. Within the AxJ region, 
we are Overweight Taiwan equities, with attractive structural 
growth driven by its semiconductor industry. We are also 
Overweight India equities, which offers diversified non-tech 
growth drivers. Taiwan and India equities both generate 
attractive returns on equity. We maintain Core allocations to 
China equities, with low valuations, and Korea equities, with 
explosive earnings growth in its semiconductors industry, but 
volatile price movements. We are Underweight ASEAN, 
which offers relatively lower earnings growth. 
What are the implications for equity sectors? 
We are Overweight these sectors globally: US 
information technology, US healthcare, US financials, US 
materials, Europe ex -UK financials, and Japan 
financials. 
A framework we use is to break the market into four key 
sector building blocks. Tech is the largest block, 
encompassing the Information Technology, Communication 
Services and Consumer Discretionary sectors. Put 
differently, the segment spans five major industries: 
semiconductors, hardware, internet, software, and IT 
services. Within the US market, we expect this segment to 
deliver c.30% earnings growth in 2027, extending the strong 
momentum seen in 2026. 
Financials and Real Estate  are the next building block, 
comprising these two sectors. We expect this segment to 
continue delivering low -teens earnings growth in 2027, 
supported by resilient economic activity and healthy credit 
trends. 
Resources include companies across four sectors: 
Industrials, Materials, Energy, and Utilities . We expect the 
segment to generate 10 -15% earnings growth in 2027, 
although Energy is likely to remain the largest drag on overall 
growth due to tougher comparisons and softer energy price 
assumptions for 2027. 
Healthcare & Others  consists primarily of Healthcare and 
Consumer Staples. We expect mid-teen earnings growth for 
the segment in 2027, with a rebound in healthcare earnings 
representing a potential upside surprise. 
Our Overweight sector exposures are drawn from each of the 
four key blocks above. Unlike the last few years , with 
concentrated gains from Tech providing market leadership, 
we see our downgrade in global equities also reflecting a 
broadening in market leadership towards each of the other 
sector blocks apart from Tech.

US and Asia ex-Japan would continue to lead in 
earnings growth in 2027 
2026 and 2027 consensus earnings growth estimates for 
MSCI equity regional indices

Source: FactSet, Bloomberg, Standard Chartered

Global equities continue to be supported by strong 
earnings growth in 2027, led by technology and

As structural themes such as AI transition from the rapid 
buildout phase towards monetisation, diversified exposure 
across sectors and regions should become increasingly 
important. 
What are the implications for equity 
opportunistic ideas? 
We have opportunistic ideas on Global High -Dividend 
equities, US healthcare, Euro area banks , MSCI 
Switzerland and Japan banks. The selection represents a 
more defensive stance  currently, with an average beta of 
below 1 relative to global equities (MSCI AC World), but 
attractive drivers nonetheless, for each of the ideas.  
Global High-Dividend offers higher-income equity allocation 
with historically lower volatility than global equities. US 
healthcare is supported by wider adoption of obesity 
treatments, advance s in oncology a nd innovative medical 
devices, that support earnings growth. Euro area banks  
continue to see steady earnings growth adding to their 
excess capital, which is being returned to shareholders via 
attractive dividend and share buyback yields. Japan banks 
continue to enjoy net margin expansion from BoJ policy 
normalisation, as well as corporate governance 
improvements. MSCI Switzerland is a newly initiated idea for 
investors seeking defensive exposure. H ealthcare and 
consumer staples account s for more than half the index 
weight, providing exposure to relatively resilient demand that 
is more insulated from economic cycles and AI trends. It also 
has a sizeable financial sector supported by steady wealth 
management growth and well-capitalised insurers with 
disciplined underwriting and resilient premium income. 
We see the case for more active management  in a mid -
cycle transition, as higher market dispersion should create 
greater opportunities for active sector and stock selection, 
particularly as leadership within equities evolves. 
What are the implications of higher equity 
market volatility?  
A more uncertain political backdrop and p otentially maturing 
growth rates in certain market segments may contribute to 
more frequent bouts of market volatility. Higher volatility 
presents an opportunity to use structured strategies to gain 
exposure to equities. Such strategies may be used to 
generate income or to gain exposure at a discounted entry 
price. Investors can also consider strategies that are 
structured to return principal or partial principal at maturity. 
What alternative strategies are suited to a 
regime of moderating equity market returns?  
Investors can consider alternative strategies such as hedge  
fund strategies or private markets, for those with the capacity

to accept some illiquidity.  
Equity long/short hedge fund  strategies would retain growth 
participation with less market exposure. Fund managers buy 
companies they expect to outperform and short those they 
expect to underperform, seeking to earn the difference 
between the two , and benefit even as total equity market 
returns are moderating. 
Within private markets , private equity provides  a means to 
higher returns with the effects of an illiquidity premium. 
Meanwhile, i nfrastructure offers  differentiated exposure to 
structural growth opportunities, where c ontracted long-term 
revenues reduce dependence on equity-market sentiment. 
What are the implications on fixed income 
and FX markets? 
Bonds: This year’s surge in bond yields means bonds now 
offer a hedge against any equity market weakness. We 
believe the 3 -7-year part of the US bond yield curve offers 
the best hedge against any equity sell -off caused by AI -
related growth worries. This part of the bond curve has priced 
in most Fed rate hike expectations, which could reverse if 
growth concerns return. Meanwhile, we favour US Treasury 
Inflation-Protected Securities (TIPS) as a hedge against any 
fuel price-led inflation scare.  
Credit: For corporate bonds, adopting a defensive stance in 
a volatile environment typically involves: i) increasing credit 
quality, favouring higher-rated issuers; and ii) moving up the 
capital structure, by reducing exposure to subordinated debt 
and favouring secured debt. From a sector perspective, 
large, diversified banks, utilities, and quasi -sovereigns tend 
to be more defensive, particularly relative to cyclical sectors, 
such as metals and mining, exploration, and technology.         
FX: We favour bearish AUD/JPY as a tactical hedge against 
any equity market correction. Review of 21 past S&P500 
correction sessions (2010 -2025) shows AUD weakened 
against USD in 86% of cases, while JPY strengthened 
against USD in 67%, supporting our bearish AUD/JPY 
outlook during any equity market stress.

tajinder.dhillon@lseg.com; 
THIS WEEK IN EARNINGS 
AGGREGATE ESTIMATES AND REVISIONS 
 26Q3 Y/Y earnings are expected to be 31.6%. Excluding the energy sector, the Y/Y earnings estimate is 26.4%. 
 Of the 299 companies in the S&P 500 that have reported earnings to date for 26Q3, 81.6% have reported earnings 
above analyst estimates. This compares to a long-term average of 67.7% and prior four quarter average of 81.5%. 
 26Q3 Y/Y revenue is expected to be 13.6%. Excluding the energy sector, the growth estimate is 12.2%. 
 69.9% of companies have reported 26Q3 revenue above analyst expectations. This compares to a long-term 
average of 63% and an average over the past four quarters of 76.8%. 
 For 26Q3, there have been 40 negative EPS preannouncements issued by S&P 500 corporations compared to 66 
positive EPS preannouncements. By dividing 40 by 66 the N/P ratio is 0.6 for the S&P 500 Index.  
 The forward four-quarter (26Q4– 27Q3) P/E ratio for the S&P 500 is 19.0. 
 During the week of Nov. 2, 144 S&P 500 companies are expected to report quarterly earnings.

There has been an increase in the share-weighted earnings for the S&P 500 since the start of the quarter (to $778.4B 
from $760.7B). Three of the eleven sectors have experienced downward revisions to estimates.

Since Aug. 1, the communication services (-20.5%) and materials (-7%) sectors have recorded the highest percentage 
decreases in earnings, while the energy (33%) sector has recorded the highest percentage increase in earnings. 
Overall, share-weighted earnings for the S&P 500 have increased by 2.3% since the start of the quarter.

Since Aug. 1, the communication services (-14.7B) and materials (-1.2B) sectors have recorded the highest dollar-level 
decreases in earnings, while the energy (15.7B) sector has recorded the highest dollar-level increase in earnings. 
Overall, expected share-weighted earnings for the S&P 500 have increased by 17.7B since the start of the quarter.

299 companies in the S&P 500 Index have reported earnings for Q3 2026. Of these companies, 81.6% reported 
earnings above analyst expectations and 15.7% reported earnings below analyst expectations. In a typical quarter 
(since 1994), 67% of companies beat estimates and 20% miss estimates. Over the past four quarters, 82% of 
companies beat the estimates and 15% missed estimates. 
In aggregate, companies are reporting earnings that are 4.5% above estimates, which compares to a long-term (since 
1994) average surprise factor of 4.4% and the average surprise factor over the prior four quarters of 7.7%.

The estimated earnings growth rate for the S&P 500 for 26Q3 is 31.6%. If the energy sector is excluded, the growth 
rate declines to 26.4%. The S&P 500 expects to see share-weighted earnings of $778.4B in 26Q3, compared to share-
weighted earnings of $591.4B (based on the year-ago earnings of the current 503 constituents) in 25Q3. 
11 of the 11 sectors in the index expect to see an improvement in earnings relative to 25Q3. The Energy and 
Information Technology sectors have the highest earnings growth rates for the quarter, while the Consumer 
Discretionary sector has the weakest anticipated growth compared to 25Q3. 
The Energy sector has the highest earnings growth rate (146.2%) of any sector. It is expected to earn $63B in 26Q3, 
compared to earnings of $25.6B in 25Q3. 4 of the 5 sub-industries in the sector are anticipated to see higher earnings 
than a year ago. The Oil & Gas Refining & Marketing (566.5%) and Integrated Oil & Gas (143.8%) sub-industries have 
the highest earnings growth in the sector. If these sub-industries are removed, the growth rate declines to 39%. 
The Information Technology sector has the second highest earnings growth rate (68.9%) of any sector. It is expected 
to earn $260.2B in 26Q3, compared to earnings of $154B in 25Q3. 12 of the 12 sub-industries in the sector are 
anticipated to see higher earnings than a year ago. The Semiconductors (137%) and Semiconductor Materials & 
Equipment (71.5%) sub-industries have the highest earnings growth in the sector. If these sub-industries are removed, 
the growth rate declines to 28.2%. 
The Consumer Discretionary sector has the lowest earnings growth rate (3.9%) of any sector. It is expected to earn 
$56.3B in 26Q3, compared to earnings of $54.2B in 25Q3. 7 of the 18 sub-industries in the sector are anticipated to 
see lower earnings than a year ago. The Casinos & Gaming (-44.1%) and Automotive Parts & Equipment (-38.8%) 
sub-industries have the lowest earnings growth in the sector. If these sub-industries are removed, the growth rate 
improves to 4.5%.

compared to earnings of $50.1B in 25Q4. 2 of the 20 sub-industries in the sector are anticipated to see lower earnings 
than a year ago. The Aerospace & Defense (-39.6%) and Passenger Airlines (-29.8%) sub-industries have the lowest 
earnings growth in the sector. If these sub-industries are removed, the growth rate improves to 30.5%.

STARMINE EARNINGS SURPRISE FORECAST
Looking forward at quarterly performance, we use StarMine’s SmartEstimate® from LSEG to determine which
companies in the S&P 500 are better poised to beat and miss earnings estimates. The SmartEstimate® is a weighted

average of analyst estimates, with more weight given to more recent estimates and more accurate analysts. Our
studies have shown that when the SmartEstimate® differs significantly from the consensus (I/B/E/S mean estimate),
the Predicted Surprise accurately predicts the direction of earnings surprises or further revisions 70% of the time.
When a significant Predicted Surprise for revenue is also present for the period, the accuracy improves to 78%.

StarMine ARM from LSEG is an analyst revisions stock ranking model, designed to predict future changes in analyst
sentiment. ARM incorporates more accurate earnings estimates through the SmartEstimate prediction service. ARM
region rankings scores companies by region on a scale of 1 to 100 where 100 represents the most bullish sentiment.
Over the next two weeks, 147 S&P 500 companies are expected to report earnings. Of these companies, 17 positive

surprises and 11 negative surprises are expected from S&P 500 companies reporting quarterly results.

### Page 2

The Week Ahead October 9, 2026 
                                                                                                                                         2

CENTRAL BANK MEETINGS

Monetary Authority of Singapore (MAS)

We expect another slight increase in the 
SGD NEER policy band slope at the MAS’ 
October review, with no change to the 
band’s width or centre. Import cost 
pressures and resilient growth favour a 
calibrated tightening, rather than a more 
forceful adjustment, in line with the 
global monetary policy stance.

Also due to be released alongside the 
MAS’ policy decision are the 3Q26 
advance GDP estimates, which we 
expect to show another quarter of strong 
growth at 5.4% yoy (1.4% qoq sa), 
compared with 5.9% yoy (1.4% qoq sa) in 
2Q26. Robust economic performance 
continued to be driven by artificial 
intelligence ( AI)-led trade activity, 
supporting the manufacturing and 
wholesale trade sectors, while strength 
in the financial sector underpinned the 
resilience of the modern services cluster. 
This came despite a moderation of the 
transport & storage sector, and 
weakness in consumer -facing food 
services and retail sectors.

FORTHCOMING DATA 
RELEASES

Malaysia

We expect Malaysia’s advance GDP 
estimate for 3Q26 to register above -
trend growth of 5.0% yoy, albeit 
moderating from 6.0% yoy  in 2Q26. The 
overall expansion continued to be 
supported by ongoing strength in the 
export-oriented manufacturing sector, 
underpinned by strong  AI-related 
demand for electronics, and sustained 
domestic demand, backed by healthy 
investment and private consumption 
trends. However, contraction in mining 
and weak agricultural activity dragged 
overall growth.

Singapore

We expect firm Singapore non -oil 
domestic exports (NODX) growth of 28.0% 
yoy in September 2026, although 
moderating from August’s exceptional 
surge of 46.2%. Although favourable 
base effects largely faded in September, 
electronics shipments likely remained 
the primary growth driver, underpinned 
by robust AI -related demand amid 
sustained global investment in AI 
infrastructure, while non-electronics 
exports were uneven.

### Page 3

The Week Ahead October 9, 2026 
                                                                                                                                         3

China

Exports growth is expected to remain 
robust at 25.7% y oy in September, 
supported by continued strength in 
electronics demand. Both the official and 
Caixin manufacturing PMIs rose above 
the 50 expansion threshold during the 
month, pointing to improving 
manufacturing activity. External trade 
indicators also stren gthened, with 
average daily deadweight tonnage 
handled at China's 20 major ports 
accelerating from -0.7% yoy in August to 
7.2% in September. Freight activity 
improved as well, as growth in 
international cargo flights picked up 
from -1.4% y oy to 3.6% over the same 
period.

On the price front, factory -gate and raw 
material prices rebounded amid the 
renewed escalation in geopolitical 
tensions. Main raw material purchasing 
price and producer price subPMI 
increased from 56.6 and 50.4 to 60.8 and 
54.0 during the period. However, 
downstream consumer price pressures 
are likely to remain relatively subdued 
under the pricing-band mechanism. As a 
result, CPI inflation is expected to edge 
up from 0.8% y oy in August to 1.0% in 
September. Nevertheless, soft domestic 
demand is expected to continue 
constraining the pace of consumer price 
increases.

India

September inflation is likely to gather 
momentum to 5.7% y/y, partly driven by 
base effects, from 4.8% the month 
before. Food segments continue to 
register gains, with pressures 
broadening to include perishables, 
edible oils, rice, pulses, sugar, etc., 
according to high-frequency data. Under 
non-food, retail pump prices have not 
been adjusted further, though other fuel 
products, such as non -subsidised LPG, 
diesel and ATF (still up by double digits 
y/y, though slower than in August), are 
up on the year. Core  inflation is also 
likely to tick up, aligning with the central 
bank's view that price risks are no longer 
benign. Pressures have built up due to 
the overhang of an uneven and sub -par 
monsoon, drought conditions in parts of 
the country, and elevated oil pr ices. In 
the face of higher headline prints, we 
expect the RBI to keep the door open for 
another rate hike in December 2026.

CENTRAL BANK MEETINGS

Bank Negara Malaysia (BNM)  (5 
November)

As written in our Macro Strategy note , 
BNM’s final meeting of 2026 on 5 
November will be live and a close call, 
after the central bank opened the door to 
monetary policy normalisation during its 
September review. BNM Governor Abdul 
Rasheed Ghaffour reiterated in a 
Bloomberg interview on 15 Oc tober that 
the central bank has space to adjust 
policy if needed, as policymakers are 
vigilant about upside inflationary 
pressures amid strong economic growth. 
He also said that policy decisions will be 
data dependent. We think the latest 
available Septemb er data for the 
upcoming meeting suggest that 
policymakers can afford to be patient 
until 1H27, maintaining the Overnight 
Policy Rate at 2.75% for now. Inflation 
remained contained within the lower half 
of the official 2026 inflation forecast 
range of 1.5 -2.5%, notwithstanding 
external price pressures and robust 
economic activity. Advance estimates 
showed GDP growth of 5.3% yoy in 3Q26, 
while exports jumped to a five -year high 
of 54.5% yoy in September, 
supercharged by the artificial 
intelligence boom. However, headline 
inflation eased slightly to 1.8% yoy in 
September 2026 from 1.9% yoy in August, 
while core inflation remained stable at 1.7% 
yoy. Fuel subsidies have limited the pass-
through of upstream cost pressures to 
consumer prices, alongside stable 
domestic demand conditions.

FORTHCOMING DATA 
RELEASES

South Korea

October trade and inflation data are due 
for release. Export growth is expected to 
accelerate to nearly 100% yoy, up from 
83.7% in September, while the trade 
surplus is forecast to remain above 
USD40bn for another month. CPI 
inflation is expected to hold steady at 2.9% 
yoy. Overall, the data should reinforce 
the view that AI - and semiconductor -
driven growth and the expansion in 
South Korea’s trade surplus remain 
firmly on track, providing fundamental 
support for the KOSPI and KRW. 
Meanwhile, inflation remains sticky 
above the BOK’s 2% target, bolstering 
the case for further monetary tightening 
as early as the November policy meeting.

Taiwan

October inflation data are forthcoming. 
Headline CPI inflation is expected to 
remain above 2% for the sixth 
consecutive month, at 2.3% yoy. Core 
CPI inflation is also likely to stay above 2% 
for the sixth consecutive month, at 2.3% 
yoy. Despite government subsidies on 
fuel and electricity prices, higher global 
energy prices continue to feed through 
to CPI via items such as airfares and tour 
package fees. Services inflation also 
remains sticky, particularly in food away 
from home, housin g, transportation, and 
entertainment. Meanwhile, domestic 
inflation expectations appear to be 
gradually rising. A recent private -sector 
survey showed that the public expects

### Page 3

The Week Ahead October 30, 2026 
                                                                                                                                         3

inflation to average 2.5% in 2026, with 
52% of respondents expecting inflation 
to exceed 2.5%. Against this backdrop, 
we maintain our view that the central 
bank will resume rate hikes in December, 
raising the policy rate by 12.5  bps to 
2.125%.

Thailand

We expect Thailand’s headline inflation 
to rise to 3.2% yoy  in October 2026, 
extending the increase over the previous 
two months, and moving slightly above 
the upper end of the Bank of Thailand’s 
1-3% target range. This was likely driven 
by faster energy inflation amid an 
uptrend in diesel prices, alongside a 
rebound in food inflation. The inflation 
uptrend was likely broadly in line with 
the central bank's expectations and 
mainly supply-side driven, supporting its 
decision to look through the temporary 
price pressures and keep its policy rate 
unchanged at its October meeting.

Vietnam

We expect Vietnam’s October 2026 
goods exports and retail sales to remain 
robust at 42.0% yoy and 14.0% yoy year-
to-date, respectively, while headline 
inflation is likely to stay elevated at 
around 5.0% yoy. Exports have 
continued to be supported by strong  
electronics and machinery shipments 
amid a favourable technology cycle with 
spillovers from robust artificial 
intelligence-related demand. Retail 
sales were likely bolstered by firm 
household spending, supported by 
favourable labour market conditions, 
and continued strength in tourism. 
Headline inflation has remained elevated 
and above the central bank’s target of 
4.5% for the year, driven by price 
pressures from transport and energy, 
food, as well as material costs. This 
poses a challenge for policymakers as 
they seek to balance inflati on risks 
against ambitions of achieving at least 10% 
economic growth this year.

Hong Kong

Hong Kong’s economy is expected to 
decelerate from 4.3% yoy in Q2 to 2.6% 
in Q3, largely due to slower private 
consumption. Despite a 13% increase in 
Mainland visitor arrivals during the 
period, domestic consumption continued 
to be weighed down by a weaken ing 
labour market, with the unemployment 
rate elevated at 3.9% in September. 
Meanwhile, higher interest rates have 
dampened investment sentiment and 
capital market activity. The Centa -City 
Leading Index remained broadly flat, 
while the Hang Seng Index was range-
bound at around 25,000. The positive 
wealth effect seen in 1H has faded as 
asset market performance remained 
muted. A strong HKD continued to 
encourage outbound tourism, evidenced 
by an increase in average daily resident 
departures from 6.9% yoy in Q2 to 10.9% 
in Q3.

However, GDP growth is expected to be 
buffered by improving net exports amid 
the ongoing AI -electronics upcycle. 
Export growth picked up and outpaced 
import growth during Q3. Hong Kong, as 
China’s free port and re -export hub, is 
benefiting from stronger de mand for 
Chinese electronic goods.

Indonesia/ Philippines

### Page 4

The Week Ahead October 30, 2026 
                                                                                                                                         4

Indonesia’s 3Q GDP likely moderated to 
5.2% y/y. Soft consumer confidence and 
retail sales growth point to weaker 
consumption, compounded by a gradual 
rise in inflation. Fiscal measures likely 
helped spending among low -income 
households. Despite the quarte rly 
moderation, we maintain our full -year 
growth forecast at 5.3% y/y.

Inflation rates in Indonesia and the 
Philippines are expected to rise, albeit to 
different degrees. Indonesia’s inflation is 
on course to edge up modestly, while in 
the Philippines, stronger pass -through 
from higher oil prices, food and 
transportation costs, as well as imported 
inflation pressures stemming from a 
weaker peso, is likely to push inflation to 
a multi-year high.

冠德 高毛利個案貢獻獲利成長 ： 冠德 1H26 合併營收 132.26 億元 ，
YoY+31.39 %，毛利率 26.22 %，較去年同期增加 2.48 個百分點，其中
2Q 單季毛利率提升至 27.81% ，主因來自於高單價及高毛利成屋個案
「冠德信義」的貢獻， 1H26 單季稅後歸屬母公司獲利 11.1 億元 ，較
去年同期成長 61.48% ，1H26 EPS 約 1.82 元；累計 01~0 9/2026 合併營
收 189.4 億元，較去年同期成長 16.94 %。 
2026 年下半年推案量 119 億元 ： 冠德預計 2026 年下半年 推出 3 個建
案，包括「台中裕毛屋案」位於台中市北屯區 ，規劃樓層 24F/B4F ，
總銷金額 67 億元， 目前 已取得使用執照， 10/2028 公開銷售 ； 「台中
G5 案」 位於台中市北屯區 ，規劃樓層 22F/B5F ，總銷金額約 16 億元，
已取得使用執照，預計 11/2026 公開銷售； 「直興案」位於台北市萬華
區，規劃樓層 21F/B5F ，總銷金額 36 億元， 10/2026 公開銷售，今年
台北及台中地區 3 個推案 合計總銷高達 119 億元。  
儲備個案量能充足： 目前冠 德 9 個儲備個案 基地面積總 計 2.23 萬 坪，
合計總銷 802 億元 ，住宅佔 66.79%、商辦佔 18.23% 及住商佔 14.98% ，
主要百億級個案近況方面， 「台電南港案」總銷 130 億元 ，目前已開
工，預計 3Q28 完工；「行 二行三 案」位於北市中正區， 總銷 221 億元，
10/2026 都市更新核定，預計 2Q27 開工；「G9-1 案」位於台中西屯區，
總銷 110 億元， 07/2025 建照審查掛件，預計 2Q27 開工；「E1E2 案」
於 10/2023 都市更新報核，預計 4Q28 開工；「專 45 案」總銷 120 億
元，12/2025 簽約， 4Q26 都更報核 ，預計 3Q28 開工。  
預期冠德配息 仍具 高殖利率 ：冠德 建案銷售率持續推展，規劃下半年
推案量能仍有百億元，目前在手 儲備個案預計總銷金額 802 億元；投
顧上調 冠德 2026/2027 年稅後 EPS 至 3.95/3.9 9 元，預估至 202 7 年底

標價 43 元( 202 7 年 PBR 1.0 倍)。

## Document 679: 国泰海通发布三季报：一个头部券商的“侧身过弯”

Source PDF: all report pdf/国泰海通发布三季报：一个头部券商的“侧身过弯”.pdf
Report source: WallstreetCN (Global)
Date: 2026-10-31
Pages: 2
Extraction status: ok

### Page 1

在⾏业整体调整的时候，姿态和份额⽐数字重要
摘要：
国内证券业的 “TOP”2 之⼀，总资产规模领先者国泰海通发布三季报。
这是证券业在今年 3 季度整体⾯临调整市道时的⼀次关键业绩报告，其中的数据和细分⾏业表现
更能反映⼀家公司的底层实⼒，⽽国泰海通显然跑出了⾃⼰的 “ 姿势 ” 。
体量登顶，业绩继续努⼒
2026 年 10 ⽉ 30 ⽇晚，合并后的国泰海通交出 2026 年的三季报。季报显⽰，公司前三季度营收
589.76 亿元，同⽐  +28.51% ；归⺟净利润  248.85 亿元，同⽐  +12.73% ；扣⾮归⺟净利润
239.47 亿元，同⽐  +46.87% 。虽然第三季度单季受市场回落等影响，营收  118.13 亿元，同⽐
-46.35% ；归⺟净利  46.25 亿元，同⽐  -27.01% ，但不妨碍公司年内⼤盘。
更 ⼤ 的 亮 点 在 资 产 规 模⽅ ⾯ ： 国 泰 海 通 三 季 度 末 的 总 资 产 达 到 2.42 万 亿 元 ， 较 年 初 增 ⻓

14.43% ，总资产、净资产、净资本三项均居⾏业第⼀ —— 这是使得国泰海通在资产规模的体量
上压过 “ ⾏业⼀哥 ” 中信证券（中信证券同期总资产 2.35 万亿）。但利润规模上，中信仍以 310.22
亿净利领先。
经纪、投⾏、资管，三⼤业务飘红
不考虑回落的单季度业绩，看前三季度累计的业务，国泰海通的财报底⾊真正显露。
经纪业务⽅向：前三季度公司依然吃到了交投回暖的红利，经纪⼿续费净收⼊  145.18 亿元，同
⽐  +34.25% ，背后是股基交易量放⼤，代理买卖证券规模扩围
投⾏业务⽅⾯：国泰海通依然是 IPO 回暖的最⼤受益机构。投⾏⼿续费净收⼊  37.39 亿元，同⽐
+42.20% ； A 股前三季度 IPO 规模同⽐⾼增，头部券商承销集中度继续抬升
资管业务⽅⾯：资管⼿续费净收⼊  58.30 亿元，同⽐  +36.43% 。公司麾下两家资管⼦公司在今
年 4 ⽉完成吸收合并交割，规模效应开始体现在费率与 AUM 上
资产负债表：袖⾥有乾坤
截 ⾄ 9 ⽉ 末 ： 国 泰 海 通 的 总 资 产  2 . 4 2 万 亿 （ 较 年 初 + 1 4 . 4 3 % ）； 归 ⺟ 净 资 产  3 7 3 2 亿

（ +12.95% ）；净资本  2212 亿（ +19.5% ）；客⼾资⾦存款  4684 亿，代理买卖证券款  6119 亿
—— 客⼾真⾦⽩银在⽤脚投票。
负债端也在加杠杆：短期借款翻倍⾄ 552 亿；交易性⾦融负债升⾄ 1386 亿；永续债新增 350 亿，
其他权益⼯具从 99 亿飙到 449 亿。
总体看，负债和资产同步扩张，反映了公司积极做⼤业务盘⼝的意愿，也让⼈看到公司放⼤业
务杠杆的结构。
此外，备受关注的投资⽅⾯，公允价值的变动成为公司过去和未来业绩的关键。总体看，公司
前三季度的 “ 公允价值变动收益 ” 还是⼤涨，同⽐ 600% 以上的增⻓，但三季度在市场回调的压⼒
